Method: 在固定LoRA微调框架下，比较22种训练时辅助损失（包括轨迹形状正则化、分布约束、预测器/目标不对称性、Fisher度量Jacobi残差、解码器可见JEPA目标等），在自然语言到正则表达式生成任务上进行系统评估，并进行Bonferroni和Holm-Bonferroni多重假设检验校正。

Result: 多个辅助项在单细胞配对检验（α=0.10）下表现显著（T3-Local最强，Δ=+2.53pp, p=0.003），但无一通过多重假设检验校正；解码器可见JEPA首次产生辅助-交叉熵梯度正余弦值，但精确匹配仍在种子噪声范围内；全参数微调复制实验在TURK（Δ=+0.04pp, p=0.96）和SYNTH（Δ=+0.52pp, p=0.28）上均确认零结果。

Conclusion: 隐藏状态表征工作与解码任务精度在该场景下弱耦合，LLM领域的JEPA评估应重新表述为耦合问题，关键问题是"在何种度量下，有用的隐藏几何结构能成为解码器可见的任务信号"。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) propose that a model should learn more useful abstractions when trained to predict latent representations rather than observed outputs. For autoregressive language-model fine-tuning the principle entails a stricter requirement: the induced hidden-state geometry must reach the language-model head \emph{and} improve the decoded task metric. We test that requirement under a fixed Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct LoRA harness on natural-language-to-regex generation, comparing twenty-two training-time auxiliaries across trajectory-shape regularisation, distributional constraints, predictor/target asymmetry, Fisher-metric Jacobi residuals, and a decoder-visible JEPA objective constructed to lie in cross-entropy's positive cone. The empirical answer is a structured null: several auxiliaries clear single-cell paired $α= 0.10$ without correction (T3-Local at $Δ= +2.53$~pp, $p = 0.003$ being the strongest), but none survives Bonferroni or Holm--Bonferroni at the relevant family-wise threshold, even though many change curvature, anisotropy, variance, and gradient direction. Decoder-visible JEPA yields the first positive auxiliary--cross-entropy gradient cosine in the study, yet exact match remains inside seed noise; a full-fine-tuning replication of the same auxiliary at $n = 5$ seeds reproduces the null on both benchmarks (TURK: $Δ= +0.04$~pp, $p_{\text{paired}} = 0.96$; SYNTH: $Δ= +0.52$~pp, $p_{\text{paired}} = 0.28$), so the null is robust across LoRA and full fine-tuning for the decoder-visible construction. Hidden-state representation work and decoded-task accuracy are therefore weakly coupled in this regime; we accordingly reframe LLM-domain JEPA evaluation as a coupling problem, in which the operative question is under which metrics useful hidden geometry becomes decoder-visible task signal.

</details>


### [116] [Breakeven complexity: A new perspective on neural partial differential equation solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15399)
*Yijing Zhang,Nicholas Roberts,Tanya Marwah,Mikhail Khodak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对神经 PDE 求解器提出了以**盈亏平衡复杂度**为核心的评估框架，该指标衡量神经求解器在成本上优于传统求解器所需的前向求解次数。通过缩放定律指导训练预算分配，并在多个 2D 周期域 PDE 及绕流基准上验证，发现问题越难（高维、长 rollout、高雷诺数等），神经求解器越有优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于精度的评估忽视了神经求解器高昂的前期成本（数据生成、训练、调参）以及传统求解器也能以低代价提供低精度解的事实，需要一种能全面计入端到端成本的评估方法。

Method: 提出**盈亏平衡复杂度**指标，即神经求解器相比误差等效的传统求解器达到成本效益平衡所需的前向求解次数；利用缩放定律确定数据生成与训练预算分配；在 APEBench 的 2D 周期域 PDE 和 PyFR 生成的多障碍绕流基准上评测多个神经 PDE 求解器。

Result: 神经 PDE 求解器在问题越困难（成本更高、维度更高、rollout 更长、物理状态更复杂如高雷诺数）时效果越好，盈亏平衡复杂度越低。

Conclusion: 神经求解器的成本效益与问题难度正相关，所提框架能为神经 PDE 求解器的实际部署提供更合理的评估依据。

Abstract: Neural surrogate solvers of partial differential equations (PDEs) promise dramatic speedups over numerical methods, especially in scenarios requiring many solves. However, current accuracy-based evaluations do not fully consider two central issues: (1) neural solvers incur substantial up-front costs for data generation, training, and tuning; and (2) classical solvers can also generate low-fidelity solutions at a sufficiently low simulation cost. To explicitly account for these realities and fully incorporate end-to-end costs, we propose an evaluation framework centered on breakeven complexity, a metric that counts the forward solves before a learned solver is cost-effective relative to an error-equivalent traditional solver. To evaluate this measure, we apply scaling laws to determine how much training budget to allocate to data generation and discuss how to achieve smooth error-matching in diverse settings. We evaluate the breakeven complexity of multiple neural PDE solvers on three PDEs on 2D periodic domains from APEBench and a novel benchmark of flows past multiple obstacles generated by the GPU-native PyFR code. Among other findings, our results suggest that neural PDE solvers become more effective as problems get harder in terms of cost, dimension, rollout, physics regime (e.g. higher Reynolds number), etc.

</details>


### [117] [Transformer Scalability Crisis: The First Comprehensive Empirical Analysis of Performance Walls in Modern Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15413)
*Mahdi Naser Moghadasi,Faezeh Ghaderi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过对118个Transformer模型在7种架构类别上的大规模系统评估，揭示了Transformer架构存在关键的性能瓶颈：序列长度从512 tokens增至1024 tokens时，模型成功率从88.1%骤降至44.9%，2048 tokens时完全失败（0%）。压缩模型在参数效率（649.2 tokens/sec/M参数）上显著优于大型生成模型（12.5 tokens/sec/M），为理论O(n²)注意力复杂度转化为实际性能墙提供了首个定量证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer架构在NLP中取得了显著成功，但其可扩展性限制尚未通过系统的实证分析得到充分理解，现有研究缺乏大规模、跨架构类别的定量评估。

Method: 对118个Transformer模型（7种架构类别）进行系统化基准测试，评估序列长度从128到2048 tokens范围内的加载时间、内存消耗和计算效率，分析模型成功率和参数效率。

Result: 512 tokens时88.1%模型成功，1024 tokens时降至44.9%，2048 tokens时完全失败（0%）。压缩模型参数效率（649.2 tokens/sec/M参数）远高于大型生成模型（12.5 tokens/sec/M）。

Conclusion: 理论O(n²)注意力复杂度在实际中转化为可测量的性能墙，建立了新的Transformer评估基准方法，为生产环境中的实际部署决策提供了关键见解。

Abstract: Despite the remarkable success of transformer architectures in natural language processing, their scalability limitations remain poorly understood through systematic empirical analysis. This paper presents the first comprehensive large-scale evaluation of 118 transformer models across seven distinct architectural categories, revealing fundamental performance walls that manifest as hard deployment constraints. Our systematic benchmarking methodology uncovers a critical scalability crisis: while 88.1% of models successfully process sequences up to 512 tokens, this drops dramatically to 44.9% at 1024 tokens, with complete failure (0%) at 2048 tokens. Through rigorous analysis of loading times, memory consumption, and computational efficiency across sequence lengths from 128 to 2048 tokens, we demonstrate that compressed models achieve superior parameter efficiency (649.2 tokens/sec/M parameters) compared to large generative models (12.5 tokens/sec/M). Our findings challenge prevailing scaling assumptions and provide the first quantitative evidence that the theoretical O(n2) attention complexity translates into measurable performance walls. This work establishes new benchmarking methodologies for transformer evaluation and provides critical insights for practical deployment decisions in production environments.

</details>


### [118] [Margin-Adaptive Confidence Ranking for Reliable LLM Judgement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15416)
*Gaojie Jin,Yong Tao,Lijia Yu,Tianjin Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种学习型置信度估计器，替代启发式置信度信号，用于保证LLM与人类判断之间的一致性。该方法利用模拟标注者多样性和基于边界的排序公式，显式建模LLM区分人类一致与不一致案例的置信度，并推导出泛化保证，揭示了边界相关的权衡。集成到固定序列测试后，该方法提升了排序准确性，增强了置信度与分歧风险之间的单调关系，在多个数据集和评估模型上实现了更高的一致性达标率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有假设检验框架依赖于模型估计置信度与人类分歧风险之间的单调性假设，但该假设在实践中可能被违反，且置信度估计器的泛化行为未被明确分析。

Method: 学习专用置信度估计器，利用模拟标注者多样性和基于边界的排序公式，显式建模LLM区分人类一致与不一致案例的置信度；推导泛化保证，揭示边界相关的权衡；设计自适应估计器训练流程，集成到固定序列测试中。

Result: 学习型置信度估计器在多个数据集和评估模型上显著提升了排序准确性，加强了置信度与分歧风险之间的单调关系，提高了目标一致性水平的达标成功率。

Conclusion: 通过学习型置信度估计器替代启发式信号，能够有效缓解单调性假设被违反的问题，并提供更强的泛化保证，从而提升LLM与人类判断一致性的保证效果。

Abstract: Jung et al. (2025) introduce a hypothesis testing framework for guaranteeing agreement between large language models (LLMs) and human judgments, relying on the assumption that the model's estimated confidence is monotonic with respect to human-disagreement risk. In practice, however, this assumption may be violated, and the generalization behavior of the confidence estimator is not explicitly analyzed. We mitigate these issues by learning a dedicated confidence estimator instead of relying on heuristic confidence signals. Our approach leverages simulated annotator diversity and a margin-based ranking formulation to explicitly model how confidently an LLM distinguishes between human-agreement and human-disagreement cases. We further derive generalization guarantees for this estimator, revealing a margin-dependent trade-off that informs the design of an adaptive estimator training procedure. When integrated into fixed-sequence testing, the learned confidence estimator yields improved ranking accuracy and empirically strengthens the monotonic relationship between confidence and disagreement risk, leading to higher success rates in satisfying target agreement levels across multiple datasets and judge models.

</details>


### [119] [$f$-Trajectory Balance: A Loss Family for Tuning GFlowNets, Generative Models, and LLMs with Off- and On-Policy Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15417)
*Jake Fawkes,Jason Hartford*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，从KL散度到$f$-散度家族的扩展中，目标与模型对数概率之间的均方误差损失可以推广到整个$f$-散度家族，在策略上其梯度对应$f$-散度的梯度，在离策略下仍保持相同的全局最优解，从而为生成模型训练提供新的替代损失函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将原本仅适用于KL散度的对数概率均方误差损失推广到整个$f$-散度家族，使得不同$f$-散度对应的模式覆盖特性能够在离策略数据上得以保留和应用。

Method: 证明了目标与模型对数概率上的平移不变损失函数与$f$-散度之间存在一一对应关系；在策略下该损失函数的梯度等价于对应$f$-散度的梯度，在离策略下仍保持相同全局最优解。

Result: 在合成示例、分子发现的SynFlowNets以及异步大语言模型调优等任务上验证了所提出损失函数在策略和离策略下均能保留对应$f$-散度的预期性质。

Conclusion: 本文提出的$f$-散度家族损失函数为生成模型训练提供了更灵活的框架，能够在保证离策略适用性的同时，实现不同散度对应的模式覆盖或模式追求等特性。

Abstract: In GFlowNets and variational inference, it has been shown that the mean square error between target and model log probabilities is an effective, low variance, surrogate loss for training generative models.
  This loss has the property that when evaluated \emph{on-policy} its gradients correspond to those of the KL divergence, while \emph{off-policy} it remains a valid loss with the same global minimizer. In this work, we demonstrate that this construction can be extended to the whole family of $f$-divergences, leading to a family of losses whose on-policy gradients are that of the corresponding $f$-divergence, but retain the same global minimizer off-policy. Specifically, we show that the on-policy gradients lead to a one to one correspondence between translation invariant loss functions on the target and model log probabilities, and $f$-divergences. This equivalence allows us to design new surrogate loss functions for tuning a wide class of generative models that inherit the properties of the corresponding $f$-divergence, such as being more mode covering, whilst being applicable to off-policy data. We apply our losses on a range of tasks, including classic synthetic examples, SynFlowNets for molecule discovery, and asynchronous large language model (LLM) tuning, demonstrating that our models retain their predicted properties on- and off-policy in a wide class of generative models.

</details>


### [120] [Lagrangian Flow Matching: A Least-Action Framework for Principled Path Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15419)
*Shukai Du,Junzhe Zhang,Yiming Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出拉格朗日流匹配（Lagrangian Flow Matching），一个基于最小作用量原理的物理框架，通过优化一般拉格朗日量下的作用量来生成概率路径和速度场，将最优传输流匹配和谐波振荡器路径作为特例纳入统一框架，并产生新的可学习的动力学路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配方法（如整流流、最优传输路径）仅使用直线路径连接耦合端点，对应经典力学中自由粒子的最简单情况（动能拉格朗日量），动力学类过于狭窄。作者希望引入更广泛的物理原理（最小作用量原理）来设计更丰富的概率路径和速度场。

Method: 提出拉格朗日流匹配框架：在连续性方程和端点约束下，通过最小化一般拉格朗日量的作用量来确定概率路径和速度场。证明了该动态问题等价于静态最优传输公式，从而得到一系列免模拟的训练目标。动能拉格朗日量特例恢复OT流匹配，谐振子拉格朗日量恢复三角方差保持扩散路径。

Result: 更一般的拉格朗日量产生了新的概率路径和速度场。数值实验表明，这些新路径在保持与现有条件流匹配模型竞争力的情况下，能够诱导学习动力学的有意义的改变。

Conclusion: 拉格朗日流匹配将流匹配与经典力学中的最小作用量原理联系起来，提供了一个统一的物理框架，能够生成更丰富的概率路径族，拓展了流匹配方法的设计空间。

Abstract: Flow matching trains a neural velocity field by regression against a target velocity associated with a prescribed probability path connecting a simple initial distribution to the data distribution. A central design choice is the path itself. Existing constructions, including rectified and optimal-transport-based paths, transport samples along straight lines between coupled endpoints and thus cover only a narrow class of dynamics. We observe that this corresponds to the simplest case of the least-action principle in classical mechanics, in which the kinetic Lagrangian yields free-particle straight-line trajectories. Building on this observation, we propose Lagrangian flow matching, a physics-based framework in which the probability path and velocity field are determined by minimizing the action of a general Lagrangian subject to the continuity equation and the prescribed endpoints. We show that this dynamic problem admits an equivalent static optimal transport (OT) formulation, yielding a family of simulation-free training objectives that recover OT-based flow matching as the kinetic special case and the trigonometric variance-preserving diffusion path as the harmonic-oscillator case. More general Lagrangians give rise to new probability paths and velocity fields, and numerical experiments show that they induce meaningful changes in the learned dynamics while remaining competitive with existing conditional flow matching models.

</details>


### [121] [Spectral Priors vs. Attention: Investigating the Utility of Attention Mechanisms in EEG-Based Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15433)
*Tawsik Jawad,Gowtham Atluri,Vikram Ravindra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于脑电信号频谱选择性特征构建的方法，通过提取主要脑波频带的信号强度，将高维原始数据转化为高价值频谱特征。实验表明：传统机器学习模型结合频域/时频域特征可达到或超越SOTA深度学习模型；注意力机制无法提取稳定的神经活动特征签名；注意力模型在频谱特征发现上的局限性具有根本性。在三个静息态和一个任务态EEG数据集上验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG时间序列存在显著噪声和粗空间分辨率，导致神经退行性疾病分类困难。即使SOTA深度学习模型也难以区分健康对照与患病受试者或不同疾病类型，原因在于组间高度相似性。

Method: 采用频谱选择性特征构建方法，分离主要脑波频带内的信号强度，将高维原始数据转换为高价值频谱特征。通过频域和时频域特征结合传统机器学习模型进行分类，并与基于注意力机制的深度学习模型进行对比。

Result: a) 频域/时频域特征使传统机器学习模型性能匹配或超越SOTA深度学习模型；b) 注意力机制无法提取表征健康神经活动的稳定特征签名；c) 注意力模型在发现相关频谱特征方面存在根本性局限，即使提供频率选择性时域输入也无法显著提升性能。

Conclusion: 频谱选择性特征构建方法能有效增强类别可分性，传统机器学习模型在EEG分类任务上具有竞争力，而注意力机制在处理EEG频谱特征方面存在根本性缺陷。

Abstract: Electroencephalograph (EEG) timeseries signals are characterized by significant noise and coarse spatial resolution, which complicates the classification of neurodegenerative diseases. Even SOTA deep learning architectures struggle to distinguish between healthy controls and diseased subjects, or between different disease types, due to high intergroup similarity. In this paper, we show that a spectrally selective approach to feature construction enhances class separability. By isolating signal strengths within the primary brainwave bands, we transform high dimensional raw data into high value spectral features. Our results demonstrate that a) features derived from frequency and time frequency domain allow traditional machine learning models to match or exceed the performance of SOTA deep learning models, b) Attention mechanism is unable to distill the stable feature signatures that characterize healthy neural activity in both resting and task EEGs, and c) the limitations of attention based models in finding relevant spectral features appear to be fundamental in that providing frequency selective time domain input do not appreciably improve their performance. We validate our methodology across three open source resting EEG datasets and one task EEG dataset, providing robust empirical evidence for our claims.

</details>


### [122] [Layer-wise Derivative Controlled Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15463)
*Rowan Martnishn,Sean Anderson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ChainzRule（CR）架构，通过多项式引擎和微分正则化（DREG）协调准确性、硬件效率和功能稳定性三大冲突目标，在参数减少15.5倍的同时提升精度并降低梯度波动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型在追求高准确率时，常以硬件效率下降和功能不稳定（输入微小变化导致输出剧烈波动）为代价，三者之间存在根本性冲突，尤其限制了在敏感环境中的实际部署。

Method: 提出ChainzRule架构，核心是用多项式引擎替代标准分段线性激活函数，并引入微分正则化（DREG）——一种对中间导数进行靶向正则化的方法，而非传统的全局Lipschitz常数粗粒度约束。

Result: 在"公平对决"基准测试中，ChainzRule以15.5倍更少的参数超越标准模型；在MNIST上平均降低23.1%的峰值梯度波动；在Yelp Full序数回归任务上达到70.17%的准确率。

Conclusion: 通过DREG将梯度感知嵌入架构设计，证明了稳定性与准确性并非互斥目标，二者可以在实际任务中兼得。

Abstract: As machine learning models grow in complexity, they increasingly struggle with three conflicting demands: the need for high accuracy, the requirement for hardware efficiency, and the necessity of functional stability. Traditional architectures often achieve performance at the expense of spiky or unpredictable behavior, where small changes in input lead to massive swings in output -- a critical flaw for real-world deployment in sensitive environments. This paper introduces ChainzRule (CR), a novel neural architecture designed to harmonize these competing goals. ChainzRule replaces standard piecewise-linear activations with a Polynomial Engine governed by Differential Regularization (DREG). Unlike traditional methods that impose global, coarse-grained constraints on a model's Lipschitz constant, DREG acts as a targeted regularization on intermediate derivatives. This approach suppresses extreme sensitivity without attenuating the representational power inherent in the Polynomial Engine. In head-to-head "Fair Fight" benchmarks, ChainzRule outperformed standard models while using 15.5x fewer parameters. On the MNIST dataset, it reduced peak gradient volatility by an average of 23.1%, ensuring a smoother and more predictable manifold. On Yelp Full ordinal regression under explicit DREG regularization, ChainzRule achieves 70.17% accuracy, validating that derivative-aware regularization is compatible with competitive performance on realistic tasks. By embedding gradient awareness into the architecture via DREG, ChainzRule demonstrates that stability and accuracy need not be competing objectives.

</details>


### [123] [GRLO: Towards Generalizable Reinforcement Learning in Open-Ended Environments from Zero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15464)
*Shangjian Yin,Yu Fu,Yue Dong,Zhouxing Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GRLO方法，在Qwen3-4B-Base骨干上仅用5K提示样本和22.7 GPU小时（比强域内RLVR基线少46倍数据、68倍计算量），通过与开放环境中的少量交互从头进行RLHF训练，使平均性能从24.1提升至63.1，甚至与Qwen官方发布的大成本后训练模型竞争力相当，证明了RLHF习得的对话能力可隐式迁移到数学推理和代码生成等下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL后训练在推理领域虽表现优异，但域内RL训练需要大量GPU计算资源，成为广泛采用的重大障碍。本文希望探索能否通过少量交互的RLHF训练，使模型习得的对话能力隐式迁移到下游任务，从而大幅降低训练成本。

Method: 在Qwen3-4B-Base骨干上，从零使用少量（5K）提示样本在开放环境中进行RLHF（从人类反馈中强化学习）训练，探究其泛化能力，并将习得的对话能力隐式迁移到数学推理和代码生成等下游任务，命名为GRLO。

Result: GRLO将平均性能从24.1提升至63.1，仅需5K提示样本和22.7 GPU小时，比强域内RLVR基线少46倍数据和68倍计算量；所得模型与Qwen官方发布的大成本后训练模型竞争力相当；后续域内RLVR阶段仅在较难的竞赛数学基准上带来选择性增益。

Conclusion: GRLO提供了一种简单高效的构建广泛能力后训练模型的方案，证明了少量交互的RLHF可以隐式泛化到多种下游任务，大幅降低了计算开销。

Abstract: Post-training has become a crucial step for unlocking the capabilities of large language models, with reinforcement learning (RL) emerging as a critical paradigm. Recent RL-based post-training has increasingly split into two paradigms: reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF), which optimizes models using human preference signals in target domains, and reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR), which operates in verifier-backed environments. The latter has dominated recent reasoning-oriented post-training because it delivers stronger gains and higher efficiency on domain-specific tasks (e.g., reasoning). However, although in-domain RL training achieves promising performance, it still requires a substantial amount of GPU compute, which remains a major barrier to broad adoption. In this work, we study the generalization ability of RLHF learned from scratch from a small set of interactions in open-ended environments, and investigate whether the conversational abilities it explicitly acquires can implicitly transfer to downstream tasks such as mathematical reasoning and code generation, namely GRLO. Specifically, on Qwen3-4B-Base backbone, GRLO improves the average performance across all domains from 24.1 to 63.1 with only 5K prompts and 22.7 GPU hours, requiring about $46\times$ less data and $68\times$ less compute than a strong in-domain RLVR baseline. The resulting model is even competitive with Qwen's released post-trained models which required a much larger training cost. Notably, a subsequent in-domain RLVR stage brings only selective gains, mainly on harder competition-math benchmarks. We hope GRLO offers a simple and efficient recipe for building broadly capable post-trained models. Our code and data will be available at: \href{https://github.com/SJY8460/GRLO}{https://github.com/SJY8460/GRLO}.

</details>


### [124] [Toward World Modeling of Physiological Signals with Chaos-Theoretic Balancing and Latent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15465)
*Yunfei Luo,Xi Chen,Yuliang Chen,Lanshuang Zhang,Md Mofijul Islam,Siwei Zhao,Peter Kotanko,Subhasis Dasgupta,Andrew Campbell,Rakesh Malhotra,Tauhidur Rahman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NormWear-2是一个面向生理时间序列信号的通用世界模型，通过将多变量生理信号与临床干预变量编码到共享潜空间并建模其联合时间演化，实现了跨多个时间尺度的长时程预测。该模型在预训练阶段通过混沌理论平衡动力学多样性来增强表征鲁棒性，在多个真实世界数据集（涵盖日常、床旁和临床场景，共计8026名受试者）上取得了优于现有时间序列基础模型的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生理时间序列建模研究主要集中在分类、事件预测或短时程单步预测等静态任务上，长时程信号级预测以及生理信号的预测性本质尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出NormWear-2世界模型：(1) 将多变量生理信号与临床干预变量编码到共享潜空间，建模其联合时间演化作为动力系统；(2) 结合先验预训练知识（直觉）与非参数潜状态转移即时自适应（洞察）；(3) 预训练阶段采用混沌理论平衡动力学多样性，通过较小的平衡语料库（为两倍大小非平衡语料的一半）获得更鲁棒的表征并捕获分岔机制。

Result: 在涵盖日常、床旁和临床场景（健身计划、血液透析、糖尿病管理、手术监测）的多个数据集上评估，共包含8026名受试者，研究时长从3.2小时到2.3年。NormWear-2在时域、频域和潜表示域均取得最佳总体预测性能，显著超越现有时间序列基础模型，同时保持具有竞争力的下游表示质量。

Conclusion: NormWear-2为迈向通用生理信号世界模型迈出了一步，验证了混沌理论平衡动力学多样性在预训练中的有效性，以及结合先验知识与即时自适应机制进行长时程预测的可行性。

Abstract: Physiological time series signals reflect complex, multi-scale dynamical processes of the human body. Existing modeling studies focus on static tasks such as classification, event forecasting, or short-horizon next step prediction, while long-horizon signal-level forecasting and predictive nature of physiological signals remain underexplored. We introduce NormWear-2, a world model that encodes both multivariate physiological signals and clinical intervention variables into a shared latent space and models their joint temporal evolution as a dynamical system. Our approach combines inference from prior pre-trained knowledge (intuition) with instant non-parametric latent state transition adaptation (insight), enabling coherent forecasting across multiple temporal scales, conditioned on heterogeneous clinical interventions. During the pretraining phase, we find that chaos-theoretic balancing of dynamical regime diversity yields more robust representations, with a smaller balanced corpus outperforming one twice its size and capturing bifurcation regimes. We evaluate the world model performance across diverse real-world physiological datasets spanning heterogeneous temporal resolutions and intervention regimes, covering daily life, point-of-care, and clinical settings, including fitness planning, hemodialysis, diabetes management, and surgical monitoring. These evaluation datasets comprise records from 8,026 subjects, spanning study durations from 3.2 hours for high-resolution signal data to 2.3 years for longitudinal clinical biomarker tracking. NormWear-2 achieves the best overall forecasting performance across time, frequency, and latent representation domains, with significant improvements over state-of-the-art time series foundation models, while maintaining competitive downstream representation quality, providing a step toward general-purpose world models for physiological signals.

</details>


### [125] [Learning Normalized Energy Models for Linear Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15487)
*Nicolas Zilberstein,Santiago Segarra,Eero Simoncelli,Florentin Guth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于协方差正则化的能量模型，解决扩散模型在逆问题中隐式先验密度和采样偏差问题，实现无需重训即可计算归一化后验密度、自适应采样、Metropolis-Hastings校正及盲估计退化算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型作为逆问题的先验模型存在两个关键局限：(1)先验密度是隐式表示的，无法直接计算；(2)依赖似然近似导致采样偏差。这两个问题限制了扩散模型在成像逆问题中的应用效果和灵活性。

Method: 训练一个基于能量的模型，采用去噪目标并引入协方差基正则化项，该正则项强制不同测量条件之间的一致性。训练后的模型可直接为各种线性逆问题计算归一化后验密度，无需额外重训或微调。

Result: 在ImageNet、CelebA、AFHQ数据集上，针对图像修复和去模糊任务，该方法性能达到或超越现有基线方法。同时实现了扩散模型此前不具备的能力：能量引导的自适应采样（可动态调整调度）、无偏的Metropolis-Hastings校正步骤、以及通过贝叶斯规则对退化算子进行盲估计。

Conclusion: 该方法在保留扩散模型采样能力的同时，解决了其隐式先验密度和采样偏差两大核心问题，为线性逆问题提供了更灵活、更准确的解决方案。

Abstract: Generative diffusion models can provide powerful prior probability models for inverse problems in imaging, but existing implementations suffer from two key limitations: $(i)$ the prior density is represented implicitly, and $(ii)$ they rely on likelihood approximations that introduce sampling biases. We address these challenges by introducing a new energy-based model trained for denoising with a covariance-based regularization term that enforces consistency across different measurement conditions. The trained model can compute normalized posterior densities for diverse linear inverse problems, without additional retraining or fine tuning. In addition to preserving the sampling capabilities of diffusion models, this enables previously unavailable capabilities: energy-guided adaptive sampling that adjusts schedules on-the-fly, unbiased Metropolis-Hastings correction steps, and blind estimation of the degradation operator via Bayes rule. We validate the method on multiple datasets (ImageNet, CelebA, AFHQ) and tasks (inpainting, deblurring), demonstrating competitive or superior performance to established baselines.

</details>


### [126] [Learning with Conflicts of Interest](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15504)
*Nischal Aryal,Arash Termehchy,Ali Vakilian,Marianne Winslett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个博弈论框架来建模机器学习系统与用户之间的利益冲突，通过可扩展算法在理论上保证最大化用户期望信息、同时最小化系统偏见和操纵行为，从而保护用户免受不良信息影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ML系统常产生偏见信息，影响用户做出不利决策。现有缓解方案依赖系统所有者主动实施去偏协议，但所有者缺乏激励（认为限制其表达自由或商业利益）。需要一种认识到利益冲突并保护用户的新方法。

Method: 提出博弈论框架来建模ML系统与用户之间的利益冲突互动；设计可扩展算法，提供理论保证，在用户与ML系统交互中最大化期望信息与行动，同时最小化偏见和操纵性行动。

Result: 提出了具有理论保证的可扩展算法，能够有效平衡信息获取与偏见最小化之间的博弈。

Conclusion: 成功的解决方案必须认识到ML系统与用户之间的利益冲突，并利用这一信息保护用户，而不是仅依赖系统所有者的自愿合规。

Abstract: Financial, social, and political factors often prevent the interests of the owners of ML systems and services and their users from being perfectly aligned. ML systems often produce biased information that can influence users to make decisions that are not in their best interest. Current solution approaches require ML systems to implement protocols to mitigate their biases. However, ML system owners usually do not have any incentive to implement these protocols and often argue that it limits their freedom of expression or business. We believe that a successful solution to this problem must recognize the conflict of interest between the ML systems and their users, and use this information to protect users against information that adversely influences their decisions while allowing users to safely benefit from these systems. To this end, we propose a game-theoretic framework that models the interaction between ML systems and users with conflicts of interest. We present scalable algorithms with theoretical guarantees that maximize the amount of desired information and actions and minimize the amount of biased and manipulative actions in interaction with ML systems.

</details>


### [127] [STS: Efficient Sparse Attention with Speculative Token Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15508)
*Ceyu Xu,Jiangnan Yu,Yongji Wu,Yuan Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STS，一种无需重新训练的稀疏注意力机制，利用小模型识别重要token来为大模型动态构建稀疏掩码，在约90%稀疏度下实现2.67倍加速，且精度损失可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力机制的二次复杂度导致LLM推理面临严重的内存和计算瓶颈，尤其在处理百万级token序列的智能体应用中更为突出。

Method: STS将投机解码框架中草稿模型的注意力分数重新利用，动态构建逐token和逐头的稀疏掩码，从而剪枝目标LLM中昂贵的注意力计算，无需模型重训练。

Result: 在NarrativeQA基准上，在约90%稀疏度下实现2.67倍加速，精度下降可忽略不计，在稀疏度-精度权衡上达到新SOTA。

Conclusion: STS通过草稿模型指导稀疏化，在保持精度的同时显著提升推理效率，优于先前技术。

Abstract: The quadratic complexity of attention imposes severe memory and computational bottlenecks on Large Language Model (LLM) inference. This challenge is particularly acute for emerging agentic applications that require processing multi-million token sequences. We propose STS, a sparse attention mechanism that requires no model retraining. STS leverages the key insight that tokens identified as important by a smaller draft model are highly predictive of important tokens for a larger target model. By integrating into speculative decoding frameworks, STS repurposes the draft model's attention scores to dynamically construct a token-and-head-wise sparsity mask. This mask effectively prunes the expensive attention computation in the target LLM. Our evaluation shows that STS achieves a 2.67x speedup operating at approximately 90% sparsity on representative benchmark NarrativeQA, maintaining negligible accuracy degradation compared to dense attention. STS establishes a new state-of-the-art on the sparsity-accuracy trade-off, outperforming prior techniques by enabling higher sparsity levels for a given accuracy budget.

</details>


### [128] [parallelcbf: A composable safety-filter and auditability framework for tensor-parallel reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15509)
*Yijun Lu,Zilei Yang,Yuyin Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ParallelCBF 是首个统一张量并行无人机环境、硬门控CBF安全滤波器、分片BC-to-RL流水线和可组合审计API的框架，支持端到端安全约束训练，具备可审计的4层API和属性测试套件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架（Isaac Lab、OmniSafe、safe-control-gym、CBFKit）各自提供无人机仿真、约束强化学习基准或控制屏障函数工具，但缺乏一个统一端到端安全约束训练与可审计性的集成框架。

Method: 提出四层可组合API架构，集成：(i) 张量并行无人机环境；(ii) 硬门控CBF安全滤波器；(iii) 分片行为克隆到强化学习流水线；(iv) 可组合审计API（预注册、看门狗注册表、故障取证、数据集审计）。提供CPU PyTorch参考实现的双屏障CBF（平方/线性预测）。

Result: 39项属性安全不变性测试套件在向量化批大小下1.67秒完成；31,415轮次行为克隆数据采集活动，课程混合、每桶产量和数据集SHA-256均可审计；端到端流水线中审计层成功阻止了未满足预注册收敛标准的下游训练阶段，防止退化检查点传播。

Conclusion: ParallelCBF证明了可审计性架构属性对于可复现的实证机器人研究是必要的，而不仅仅是有用的。框架以Apache 2.0许可证发布，可通过pip install parallelcbf安装。

Abstract: While Isaac Lab provides massive parallel UAV simulation, OmniSafe and safe-control-gym provide constrained-RL benchmarks, and CBFKit provides control-barrier-function synthesis tooling, no existing framework unifies these capabilities for end-to-end safety-constrained training. ParallelCBF is the first framework to unify (i)~tensor-parallel UAV environments, (ii)~hard-gate CBF safety filters, (iii)~sharded BC-to-RL pipelines, and (iv)~first-class operational auditability -- pre-registration, watchdog registries, failure forensics, and dataset audits as composable APIs rather than user-implemented scripts. We release ParallelCBF v0.1.0 under Apache~2.0 with a four-layer composable API, a CPU PyTorch reference implementation of a dual-barrier (squared / linear-predictive) CBF, property-based safety invariance tests across vectorized batch sizes that complete in 1.67~s for the full 39-test suite, and a 31{,}415-episode behavior-cloning collection campaign whose curriculum mix, per-bucket yields, and dataset SHA-256 are auditable through the framework's own \texttt{ops} primitives. We report a representative end-to-end pipeline execution in which the framework's auditability layer halted a downstream training stage that did not meet pre-registered convergence criteria, preventing silent propagation of a degraded checkpoint -- an architectural property we argue is necessary, not merely useful, for reproducible empirical robotics research. The framework is installable via \texttt{pip install parallelcbf}; source and release artifacts are available at https://github.com/xiaoyang-123-cell/ParallelCBF.

</details>


### [129] [On the Fragility of Data Attribution When Learning Is Distributed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15520)
*Xian Gao,Bo Hui,Min-Te Sun,Wei-Shinn Ku*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一种新的攻击面：在分布式训练中，单一参与者可通过注入少量合成数据批次的潜在优化方法，在保持全局效用的同时显著虚增其归属度值，并重塑良性客户端间的归属结构，而现有基于几何的防御无法检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据归属（Data Attribution）已在机器学习管线的定价、审计和治理中广泛应用，但现有方法隐含假设归属值能忠实反映参与者的贡献。作者质疑这一假设的可靠性，探究参与者能否在保持全局效用的同时操纵自己的归属度。

Method: 提出"归属优先攻击"（Attribution-First Attack），利用潜在优化（Latent Optimization）注入少量合成小批次数据，利用非独立同分布（non-IID）标签覆盖和评估器敏感性来最大化攻击者的归属值，同时保持全局效用不变。

Result: 在多种数据集、模型和边际效用评估器下，该攻击一致性地增加了攻击者的归属度值，改变了良性客户间的相对归属结构，且不降低模型精度，也不触发基于几何的防御机制。

Conclusion: 归属本身构成了一个新的攻击面，亟需开发对归属具有鲁棒性且激励兼容的评分机制。

Abstract: Data attribution has become an important component of pricing, auditing, and governance in machine learning pipelines, yet most attribution methods implicitly assume that attribution values faithfully reflect participants' contributions. We show that this assumption can fail: a single participant in a standard distributed training workflow can substantially inflate its measured attribution value while preserving global utility. Our attribution-first attack uses latent optimization to inject small synthetic batches that preserve utility while exploiting non-IID label coverage and evaluator sensitivities. Across datasets, models, and multiple marginal-utility evaluators, the attack consistently increases the adversary's attribution value and reshapes the relative attribution structure among benign clients without degrading accuracy or triggering geometry-based defenses. These results show that attribution itself forms a new attack surface and motivate the development of attribution-robust and incentive-compatible scoring mechanisms.

</details>


### [130] [Neural Point-Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15524)
*Bruno Trentini,Jacob Hume,Vincenzo Antonio Isoldi,Philipp Misof,Ekaterina S. Ivshina,Kelly Maggs*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经点形式（NPFs），一种基于扩散几何和拉普拉斯技术的可学习几何特征，用于点云上微分形式的比较与表示，通过形式比较矩阵实现置换不变性，并在理论和实验上验证了其有效性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点云学习中，现有方法（坐标、距离、图邻域）无法充分捕捉底层几何对象（如流形）的高阶切触信息（微分形式），亟需一种能编码更高阶几何信息的可学习特征表示。

Method: 利用扩散几何中的拉普拉斯技术，在点云上构建离散模型，通过内积比较微分形式；将子流形表示为比较矩阵，刻画特征形式对与外蕴切触信息的交互；证明了在标准采样、带宽、密度和流形假设下比较矩阵的长程一致性；设计了一种紧凑、高效、置换不变的神经层，输出学习到的形式比较矩阵。

Result: 在合成数据和生物相关实验中，NPFs提供了具有竞争力的可解释表示，在标签依赖于采样密度、流形结构或群体几何的场景中表现最为突出。

Conclusion: NPFs为点云学习提供了一种新的可学习几何特征范式，通过微分形式的视角有效捕捉了传统方法忽略的高阶几何信息，具有良好的理论保证和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Point cloud learning often rests on the premise that observed samples are noisy traces of an underlying geometric object, such as a manifold embedded in a high-dimensional feature space. Yet much of this geometry is not captured directly by coordinates, pairwise distances, or learned graph neighborhoods alone. In the smooth setting, differential forms are devices to encode higher order tangency information. In this work, we introduce a new family of principled learnable geometric features for point clouds called neural point-forms (NPFs). In the absence of a natural tangency structure, we instead use Laplacian-based techniques from Diffusion Geometry to build a discrete model for comparing differential forms on point clouds via inner products. In the continuum, submanifolds of a shared ambient feature space are represented as comparison matrices, whose entries describe how pairs of feature forms interact with extrinsic tangency information. We make this intuition precise by proving the long-run consistency of comparison matrices under standard sampling, bandwidth, density, and manifold-hypothesis assumptions. This yields a compact, efficient and permutation-invariant neural layer whose output is a learned form-comparison matrix. Across synthetic and biologically relevant experiments, we show that NPFs provide a competitive, and interpretable representation, with the strongest benefits appearing when labels depend on sampling density, manifold-like structure, or response-relevant population geometry.

</details>


### [131] [DeltaPrompts: Escaping the Zero-Delta Trap in Multimodal Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15532)
*Jaehun Jung,Hyunwoo Kim,Brandon Cui,Ximing Lu,David Acuna,Prithviraj Ammanabrolu,Yejin Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DeltaPrompts，通过识别并利用师生模型间的回答分歧（Δ）来生成高分歧度训练提示，解决了VLM蒸馏中零增量提示导致学习信号不足的问题，在多个推理基准上取得高达15%的相对改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM蒸馏中，提示通常通过简单启发式或现成数据集选取，但高达69%的提示对师生模型而言是零增量（即输出分布完全相同），导致训练信号微弱，学生模型性能随数据规模扩增迅速饱和。

Method: 提出DeltaPrompts：基于蒸馏最小化分布散度的第一性原理，通过答案分歧（Δ）量化师生模型间的功能能力差距，并设计分阶段合成流水线，以现有数据集为种子主动针对学生失败模式生成高分歧度提示。

Result: 在三个设置（同策略蒸馏、跨模型族迁移、非推理模型微调）中，DeltaPrompts均带来显著提升，在10个基准上平均高达15%相对改进，即使对高度优化的推理模型（如Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking）也有效。

Conclusion: 非零分歧是蒸馏有效扩展的关键，DeltaPrompts通过主动生成高分歧训练数据可显著提升VLM推理能力。

Abstract: Distillation enables compact Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to obtain strong reasoning capabilities, yet the prompts driving this process are typically chosen via simple heuristics or aggregated from off-the-shelf datasets. We reveal a critical inefficiency in this approach: up to 69% of the prompts in standard chart / document reasoning datasets are effectively zero-delta, meaning the teacher and student already induce the exact same answer distribution. Training on these prompts provides minimal learning signal, causing student improvement to rapidly saturate regardless of data scale. To escape the zero-delta trap, we return to first principles: distillation fundamentally minimizes distributional divergence, and thus a prompt is valuable only if it exposes a functional capability gap between the teacher and student. We quantify this gap through answer divergence ($Δ$), demonstrating that non-zero divergence is critical for effective scaling. Building on this insight, we propose a staged synthesis pipeline that repurposes existing datasets as seeds, actively targeting student failure modes to produce better prompts. The result is DeltaPrompts, a diverse dataset of 200k synthetic, high-divergence reasoning problems. We evaluate DeltaPrompts across three distinct settings: on-policy distillation with the target teacher-student pair, transfer to a novel model family without regenerating the data, and off-policy fine-tuning of a non-reasoning model. Across all scenarios, DeltaPrompts drives substantial gains, yielding up to 15% relative improvement even on top of a highly-optimized reasoning model (e.g., Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking) -- averaged over 10 benchmarks spanning chart, document and perception-centric reasoning.

</details>


### [132] [VSPO: Vector-Steered Policy Optimization for Behavioral Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15604)
*Xuechen Zhang,Zijian Huang,Kai Yang,Weijia Zhang,Jiasi Chen,Samet Oymak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VSPO通过引入行为引导向量，在策略优化过程中对稀有行为进行上采样，解决了多目标优化中的稀疏奖励问题，在多个推理基准上同时提升了目标行为控制能力和任务准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在优化主要准确率目标的同时，还需满足多样的行为偏好（如冗长度、亲和力、专业程度等），但基模型往往极少展现这些目标行为，形成稀疏的行为奖励瓶颈，亟需一种能够有效处理这种多目标优化问题的方法。

Method: VSPO修改GRPO，通过行为引导向量控制生成轨迹的行为强度，采样不同引导强度的轨迹。该过程可解释为一种在策略的潜在自蒸馏机制，使模型内化引导向量。通过变化引导强度，VSPO对稀有行为进行上采样，丰富轨迹多样性，从而缓解稀疏奖励问题并加速策略优化。

Result: 在MATH和MMLU-Pro等多个推理基准上，针对解释专业性、置信度表达、对误导上下文的鲁棒性和响应冗长度四种目标行为，VSPO在保持或提升任务准确率的同时，持续改善了目标行为的控制能力，优于奖励塑造、教师轨迹蒸馏和基于引导的基线方法。

Conclusion: VSPO通过引导向量和多样化的行为强度采样，有效解决了多目标优化中的稀疏行为奖励问题，理论分析和实验均证明其相比奖励塑造等方法具有更优的迭代复杂度和行为控制效果。

Abstract: Modern language models often need to optimize a primary accuracy objective while also accommodating secondary behavioral preferences, such as verbosity, agreeableness, or the level of technical expertise in its response. In practice, a base model may exhibit a desired behavior very rarely or not at all. Thus, endowing the model with a target behavior creates a sparse behavioral reward bottleneck. To address such multi-objective problems, we introduce Vector-Steered Policy Optimization (VSPO) which employs a steering vector associated with the target behavior to control the behavior intensity of the generated rollouts. VSPO is obtained by modifying GRPO to sample rollouts with varying steering intensities. This process can be interpreted as an on-policy latent self-distillation procedure where the model internalizes its steering vector. By varying steering intensities, VSPO upsamples rare behaviors and enriches rollout diversity, which alleviates the sparse reward issue and provably accelerates the policy optimization. Through comprehensive theory and experiments, we establish that VSPO has favorable properties compared to vanilla reward shaping and other alternative approaches. Specifically, under a bandit abstraction, VSPO provably achieves better iteration complexity than reward-shaped GRPO when the steering-induced distributions are sufficiently aligned with the target behavior. We evaluate VSPO across multiple reasoning benchmarks, including MATH and MMLU-Pro, for four target behaviors: explanation expertise, confidence expression, robustness to misleading context, and response verbosity. Our results show that VSPO consistently improves the control along target behavior while maintaining or improving task accuracy compared with reward shaping, teacher-trace distillation, and guidance-based baselines.

</details>


### [133] [CTF4Nuclear: Common Task Framework for Nuclear Fission and Fusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15549)
*Stefano Riva,Carolina Introini,Antonio Cammi,Dean Price,Alexey Yermakov,Yue Zhao,Philippe M. Wyder,Judah Goldfeder,Jan Williams,Amy Sara Rude,Matteo Tomasetto,Joe Germany,Joseph Bakarji,Georg Maierhofer,Miles Cranmer,J. Nathan Kutz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向核工程领域的机器学习通用任务框架（CTF），通过标准化的数据集、12项评估指标和从稀疏测量进行系统监测的新范式，对多种ML方法进行公平比较和基准测试，旨在提高该领域科学ML的严谨性和可重复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核能系统设计运行难度大，高保真仿真计算成本高不适用于实时场景，基于模型的方法因简化假设与真实测量存在偏差；同时数据驱动方法种类繁多，在核工程这一安全关键领域亟需对不同的ML方法进行公平比较并明确其优缺点。

Method: 构建通用任务框架（CTF），包含来自不同核系统及核相关系统的精心策划的数据集，采用12项已确立的评估指标以及基于稀疏测量的系统监测新范式，通过基准ML基线进行示例性评估。

Result: 通过基准ML基线在数据集上的测试，揭示了当前方法的局限性。

Conclusion: 期望用标准化评估（隐藏测试集）替代临时性比较，提升核工业中科学ML的严谨性和可重复性。

Abstract: The demand for clean energy is ever increasing, with new nuclear technologies presenting a complementary solution to renewable energies. However, designing and operating these systems is exceptionally difficult, given the complexity of the physical phenomena that interact to form the system dynamics. While high-fidelity simulations help to understand the non-linear, multi-physics interactions within a reactor, they are computationally expensive and rarely suitable for real-time applications. Furthermore, model-based approaches are inherently sensitive to simplifying assumptions required to derive their governing equations and parameters, leading to inevitable discrepancies with real-world measurements. In contrast, Machine Learning (ML) methods have the potential to generate reliable surrogate models which may be able to quickly predict the system's behaviour. However, the number of data-driven methods that can potentially be used for this task is large and diverse. In a safety-critical setting such as nuclear engineering, a fair comparison of different ML methods, and a clear understanding of their advantages and limitations, is of paramount importance. To address this, we introduce a Common Task Framework (CTF) for ML in nuclear engineering, building upon previous efforts in dynamical systems and seismology. This CTF considers a curated set of datasets from different nuclear and nuclear-adjacent systems. The CTF evaluates the performance of a method on 12 established metrics, alongside a new paradigm focused on system monitoring from sparse measurements only. We illustrate the framework by benchmarking standard ML baselines against these datasets, revealing current method limitations. Our vision is to replace ad hoc comparisons with standardized evaluations on hidden test sets, raising the bar for rigour and reproducibility in scientific ML for the nuclear industry.

</details>


### [134] [CrystalBoltz: End-to-End Protein Structure Determination via Experiment-Guided Diffusion for X-Ray Crystallography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15564)
*Minseo Kim,Huanghao Mai,Jay Shenoy,Alec Follmer,Gordon Wetzstein,Frederic Poitevin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CrystalBoltz是一个生成式框架，将晶体学精修转化为贝叶斯推断，直接基于结构因子振幅进行操作，从预训练先验迁移到实验引导的后验采样，在多个数据集上实现了更低的坐标RMSD和R因子，并将运行时间缩短了33倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成模型基于公共蛋白质结构数据库训练，但不能直接利用新晶体学实验的测量数据；晶体学中结构因子振幅本身无法确定电子密度图或原子结构，因为相位未被观测到且需要推断，结构确定仍然是一个逆问题，需要人工专家进行大量手动精修。

Method: CrystalBoltz将晶体学精修建模为贝叶斯推断问题，直接基于结构因子振幅操作；从预训练的蛋白质结构先验出发，进行实验引导的后验采样，然后进行原子坐标和B因子精修。

Result: 在多个蛋白质晶体学数据集上，CrystalBoltz相比最强基线实现了更低的坐标RMSD和更低的R因子，同时将运行时间减少了33倍。

Conclusion: CrystalBoltz提供了一种有效且高效的方法，将实验信息直接融入预测和精修流程，显著提升了晶体学结构确定的性能并大幅降低了计算成本。

Abstract: Generative models trained on public databases of protein structures, most of which have been determined by X-ray crystallography, now provide powerful priors for structure prediction. However, they are not readily conditioned on the measurements from a new crystallographic experiment, limiting their use for X-ray structure determination. In crystallography, the measured structure-factor amplitudes do not by themselves determine an electron density map or atomic structure because the associated phases are unobserved and must be inferred. Structure determination therefore remains an inverse problem in which candidate models must be both structurally plausible and consistent with measured diffraction data, often requiring substantial manual refinement by human experts. Emerging methods aim to incorporate experimental information more directly into predictive and refinement workflows. We present CrystalBoltz, a generative framework that casts crystallographic refinement as Bayesian inference over atomic structures and operates directly on structure-factor amplitudes. CrystalBoltz moves from unguided generation with a pre-trained prior over protein structures to experiment-guided posterior sampling, followed by atomic coordinate and B-factor refinement. Across multiple protein crystallography datasets, CrystalBoltz attains lower coordinate RMSD and lower R-factors than the strongest baselines considered, while reducing runtime by a factor of 33 relative to existing experimentally guided refinement.

</details>


### [135] [AstraFlow: Dataflow-Oriented Reinforcement Learning for Agentic LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15565)
*Haizhong Zheng,Yizhuo Di,Jiahui Wang,Shuowei Jin,Xueshen Liu,Yongji Wu,Z. Morley Mao,Ion Stoica,Jiawei Zhao,Beidi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AstraFlow是一个数据流导向的强化学习系统，通过解耦 rollout服务、数据流管理和训练组件，支持多策略协作训练、弹性缩放、异构跨区域执行和可组合数据算法，在无需系统级代码修改的情况下，相比现有系统实现2.7倍训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL系统采用以trainer为中心的控制架构，缺乏原则性的组件抽象，导致每项新扩展都需要专门的系统工程，使得agentic RL成本高昂且难以扩展至复杂工作负载（如多策略协作训练、弹性异构跨区域计算等）。

Method: 提出数据流导向的RL系统AstraFlow，用原则性组件抽象替代传统的以trainer为中心的控制架构，将rollout服务、数据流管理和训练解耦为自主组件，原生支持复杂的多策略agentic RL工作负载。

Result: 在数学、代码、搜索和AgentBench等工作负载上，同一系统无需系统级代码更改即可支持多策略训练、弹性缩放、异构跨区域执行和可组合数据算法。在多策略协作训练中，AstraFlow达到相当或更优的准确率，同时训练时间加速2.7倍。

Conclusion: AstraFlow通过数据流导向的设计有效解决了现有RL系统的扩展性瓶颈，为agentic RL提供了高效、灵活且可组合的系统架构。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is increasingly used to improve the reasoning, coding, and tool-use capabilities of large language models, but agentic RL remains prohibitively expensive. Scaling RL to agentic LLMs requires supporting complex workloads, including multi-policy collaborative training, while efficiently using elastic, heterogeneous, and cross-region compute resources. Existing LLM RL systems support some of these capabilities, but each new extension often requires dedicated system engineering. This burden arises from trainer-centered control architectures and the lack of principled abstractions for RL system components. To address these limitations, we propose AstraFlow, a dataflow-oriented RL system that replaces conventional trainer-centered control with principled component abstractions. In AstraFlow, rollout services, dataflow management, and training are decoupled into autonomous components, enabling the system to natively support complex multi-policy agentic RL workloads and efficiently exploit diverse compute resources. We evaluate AstraFlow across math, code, search, and AgentBench workloads, showing that the same system supports multi-policy training, elastic scaling, heterogeneous cross-region execution, and composable data algorithms without system-level code changes. In multi-policy collaborative training, AstraFlow achieves comparable or better accuracy than existing RL systems while speeding up training time by 2.7x.

</details>


### [136] [Gaussian Relational Graph Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15575)
*Zezhong Ding,Jin Li,Xugang Wang,Xike Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GelGT，一种高斯关系图变换器，通过结构-语义协同采样和高斯图注意力机制，在关系图学习中同时建模结构、语义和时间信息，实现高达13.8%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关系图学习方法在消息传递中因信息衰减难以捕获长程依赖，且已有关系图变换器未能联合建模结构、语义和时间信息三者。

Method: 提出GelGT，包含结构-语义协同采样策略（保留结构连通性同时过滤无关语义信息）和高斯图注意力机制（在采样子图上引入可学习高斯偏置动态编码时间依赖）。

Result: 在多种真实数据集上达到最先进的下游任务性能，预测性能提升高达13.8%。

Conclusion: GelGT通过联合建模结构、语义和时间信息，有效解决了长程依赖捕获问题，并取得显著性能提升。

Abstract: Relational graph learning models relational databases as graphs and has demonstrated superior performance on a wide range of relational predictive tasks. However, existing methods struggle to capture long-range dependencies due to information decay in their message-passing mechanisms, and recent relational graph transformers remain limited in jointly modeling structural, semantic, and temporal information. In this paper, we propose GelGT, a Gaussian relational graph transformer that explicitly addresses these challenges. GelGT introduces a structure-semantic collaborative sampling strategy to preserve structural connectivity while filtering irrelevant semantic information, and incorporates a Gaussian graph attention mechanism with a learnable Gaussian bias on the sampled subgraphs to dynamically encode temporal dependencies. Extensive experiments on various real-world datasets demonstrate that GelGT achieves state-of-the-art downstream task performance, with up to a 13.8% improvement in predictive performance.

</details>


### [137] [Embracing Biased Transition Matrices for Complementary-Label Learning with Many Classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15586)
*Tan-Ha Mai,Chao-Kai Chiang,Han-Hwa Shih,Gang Niu,Masashi Sugiyama,Hsuan-Tien Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BICL框架，通过设计非均匀（有偏）的互补标签生成过程，将互补标签限制在类别子集内，克服了互补标签学习在大规模分类任务中的长期瓶颈，在CIFAR-100和TinyImageNet-200上实现了传统方法七倍以上的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统互补标签学习方法假设标签生成过程是均匀的，这在大类别数场景下会严重稀释学习信号，导致方法难以扩展到大规模标签空间。本文旨在解决这一长期存在的瓶颈问题。

Method: 提出Bias-Induced Constrained Labeling (BICL)框架，核心思想是刻意设计有偏（非均匀）的互补标签生成过程，将互补标签限制在类别的一个子集内，而非均匀地分布于所有类别。该框架涵盖了从数据收集到模型训练的完整流程。

Result: 在CIFAR-100和TinyImageNet-200数据集上，BICL实现了传统方法七倍以上的准确率提升，验证了有偏标签生成策略在大规模分类任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为互补标签学习在大类别数场景下的实际应用开辟了新方向，证明了通过有偏设计互补标签生成过程可以有效突破该领域长期存在的扩展性瓶颈。

Abstract: Complementary-label learning (CLL) is a weakly supervised paradigm where instances are labeled with classes they do not belong to. Despite a decade of research, CLL methods remain competitive mainly on 10-class classification, with scaling to large label spaces continuing to be an enduring bottleneck. This limitation stems from the common assumption of uniform label generation in traditional methods, which fatally dilutes the learning signal in many-class settings. In this paper, we demonstrate that this long-standing barrier can be overcome by deliberately designing a biased (non-uniform) generation process that restricts complementary labels to a subset of classes. This finding motivates us to propose Bias-Induced Constrained Labeling (BICL), a principled framework spanning data collection to training that leverages this bias. BICL enables effective learning on CIFAR-100 and TinyImageNet-200, achieving more than sevenfold accuracy improvements over traditional methods. Our findings establish a new trajectory for making CLL feasible for many classes in real-world applications.

</details>


### [138] [Offline Reinforcement Learning with Universal Horizon Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15603)
*Hojun Chung,Junseo Lee,Songhwai Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通用视界模型（UHM），通过直接预测任意视界下的未来状态并采用缩尾视界分布稳定训练，在100个OGBench任务上超越现有离线强化学习基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的离线强化学习因在自生成状态上重复进行模型推理而产生复合误差。几何视界模型虽能缓解此问题，但在精确建模遥远未来状态方面仍存在挑战。

Method: 提出通用视界模型（UHM），作为GHM的推广，直接预测任意视界下的未来状态；并采用缩尾视界分布（winsorized horizon distribution）来限制过大的视界，以稳定训练过程。

Result: 在100个OGBench挑战性任务上，所提方法优于竞争性基线，特别是在数据集高度次优和需要长视界推理的任务上表现突出。

Conclusion: UHM通过灵活选择预测视界并裁剪极端视界值，有效解决了基于模型的离线强化学习中的复合误差问题，在长视界和次优数据场景下展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (RL) offers a compelling approach to offline RL by enabling value learning on imagined on-policy trajectories. However, it often suffers from compounding errors due to repeated model inference on self-generated states. While geometric horizon models (GHM) alleviate this issue through direct prediction over a discounted infinite-horizon future, they remain challenged in accurately modeling distant future states. To this end, we introduce universal horizon models (UHM), a generalization of GHM that directly predicts future states under arbitrary horizons. Leveraging this flexibility, we propose a scalable value learning method that employs a winsorized horizon distribution to stabilize training by capping excessively large horizons. Experimental results on 100 challenging OGBench tasks demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms competitive baselines, particularly on tasks with highly suboptimal datasets and those requiring long-horizon reasoning. Project page: https://rllab-snu.github.io/projects/UHM/

</details>


### [139] [Layer Equivalence Is Not a Property of Layers Alone: How You Test Redundancy Changes What You Find](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16234)
*Gabriel Garcia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了Transformer层间两种等价性测试（替换测试与互换测试）存在系统性差异，且这种差异随模型训练、规模增长而扩大，直接影响剪枝决策的安全性与成本评估；提出在剪枝前应同时评估两种swap-KL指标作为诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者发现现有工作中对Transformer层"等价性"的定义混淆不清，不同的测试协议（替换vs互换）会导致截然不同的剪枝安全评估结论，迫切需要系统性量化这种协议差异及其对模型压缩的影响。

Method: 在Pythia（410M/1.4B）、Qwen3-8B、Llama-3.1-8B等多尺度检查点上，系统测量替换测试（Replacement）和互换测试（Interchange）两种swap-KL距离，追踪从初始化到收敛的训练轨迹，并对比两种协议指导下的层移除效果。

Result: 1）替换-互换间隙从初始化到收敛持续增长；2）Qwen3-8B上互换引导的剪枝比替换引导安全数倍，而Llama-3.1-8B两协议在剪枝代价上持平，说明指标差异不必然映射到移除效果差异；3）提出剪枝前应同时计算两种swap-KL作为诊断。

Conclusion: 替换和互换测试是两种不等价的层等价性协议，协议差异会显著影响层剪枝/合并的安全性评估，建议在剪枝前使用两种swap-KL指标进行诊断，且该诊断仅需无标签前向传播。

Abstract: When researchers ask whether two transformer layers are "equivalent" for compression, they often conflate distinct tests. Replacement asks whether one layer's map can substitute for another's in place; interchange asks whether two layers approximately commute when their positions are swapped. Both are output-grounded swap-KL probes, but they need not agree: on pretrained transformers the protocol gap can change which layers look safe to prune by several-fold under the same evaluator, especially when replacement distances are high.
  We measure both protocols across checkpoints and architectures. On a Pythia training trajectory (410M and 1.4B), the replacement-interchange gap grows from initialization to convergence. Under one matched WikiText-2 contract at 8B scale, Qwen3-8B enters a divergent regime: interchange-guided removal is several-fold safer than replacement-guided at the same layer budgets, while Llama-3.1-8B ties the two protocols for pruning cost even though interchange KL is lower, showing metric gaps need not map one-to-one to removal. Before layer removal or merging, score both swap-KLs on the target checkpoint; the diagnostic requires only unlabeled forward passes.

</details>


### [140] [Transformer-like Inference from Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15608)
*Aditya Kudre,Heng-Sheng Chang,Prashant G. Mehta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从最优控制理论的第一性原理出发，推导出与decoder-only transformer等价的推理架构，证明其层操作是控制理论中dual filter的自然结果，并通过数值实验揭示transformer在嵌入维度不足时会隐式利用非马尔可夫结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从最优控制理论的第一性原理重新推导与decoder-only transformer等价的推理架构，为理解transformer的工作原理提供新的理论视角。

Method: 将预测目标重新表述为最优控制问题，针对两种模型类（离散值非线性模型和线性高斯模型）求解，得到具有类似decoder-only transformer层结构的显式推理算法——dual filter。

Result: 数值实验对比了最优控制权重与训练好的transformer的注意力权重，发现在嵌入维度不足时，transformer会隐式利用非马尔可夫结构。

Conclusion: Transformer的层操作可以视为最优控制理论中dual filter的自然结果，这为理解transformer提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Decoder-only transformers compute the conditional probability of the next token from a sequence of past observations. This paper derives, from first principles, inference architectures that solve the same prediction problem - and in doing so, recovers transformer-like layer operations as a consequence of optimal control theory. The framework is developed for two model classes: a nonlinear model of discrete-valued processes, directly motivated by the transformer, and a linear Gaussian model as a tractable baseline. For both model classes, the prediction objective is reformulated as an optimal control problem whose solution yields an explicit inference algorithm, the dual filter, with a layer structure that mirrors the layer structure of a decoder-only transformer. Numerical experiments provide a comparison of the optimal control to attention weights from a trained transformer. These experiments reveal that when the embedding dimension is insufficient, the transformer implicitly exploits non-Markovian structure.

</details>


### [141] [Position: Zeroth-Order Optimization in Deep Learning Is Underexplored, Not Underpowered](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15622)
*Sijia Liu,Yicheng Lang,Soumyadeep Pal,Changsheng Wang,Yancheng Huang,Chongyu Fan,James Diffenderfer,Bhavya Kailkhura,Yihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出零阶（ZO）优化在深度学习中被低估，其局限性源于短视的开发实践而非固有缺陷，并主张围绕其独特优势（前向计算、通信高效、资源节约）重新设计算法、系统和评估体系，开辟大规模高效学习的新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ZO优化因估计器方差大和查询复杂度高而被认为无法规模化，作者认为这一结论有误——ZO优化的局限性源于"全空间、逐元素、以估计器为中心"的短视开发实践，而非方法本身能力不足。

Method: 从算法、系统和评估三个层面提出六个立场：(1) 重新审视估计器中心的ZO方法边界（方差控制、方差-查询权衡、方向导数视角）；(2) 利用子空间和谱视角实现可解释的方差缩减与优雅的查询缩放；(3) 利用ZO的前向计算特性实现通信高效、流水线友好、资源受限的训练；(4) 厘清ZO评估与任务复杂度的关系。

Result: 揭示了ZO优化在方差控制、查询复杂度缩放、系统级通信效率和资源节约方面的未充分探索潜力，指出通过子空间方法、前向计算优势等可突破现有瓶颈。

Conclusion: 呼吁重新审视ZO优化，围绕其独特优势（而非弥补其相对于一阶方法的劣势）进行设计，ZO优化有望成为大规模、系统感知、资源高效学习的可行路径。

Abstract: Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization, learning from finite differences of function evaluations without backpropagation, has recently regained attention in deep learning due to its memory efficiency and applicability to gray- or black-box pipelines. Yet, ZO methods are often dismissed as fundamentally unscalable because of estimator variance and unfavorable query complexity. We argue that this conclusion might be misguided: ZO optimization is underexplored, not underpowered. We show that many perceived limitations stem from myopic development practices, most notably full-space, element-wise, estimator-centric designs. We articulate six positions spanning the algorithmic, systems, and evaluation stack. First, we revisit the feasibility boundaries of estimator-centric ZO methods through variance control, variance-query tradeoffs, and directional-derivative lenses. Then, we identify three underexplored opportunities: (i) subspace and spectral views of ZO that enable interpretable variance reduction with graceful query scaling, (ii) the forward-only nature of ZO as a systems advantage for communication-efficient, pipeline-friendly, and resource-constrained training, and (iii) the need to de-obfuscate ZO evaluations from task complexity. We strongly advocate rethinking ZO optimization around its unique strengths and acting accordingly, opening a viable path toward large-scale, system-aware, and resource-efficient learning with ZO optimization.

</details>


### [142] [IO-SVD: Input-Output Whitened SVD for Adaptive-Rank LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15626)
*Ali Abbasi,Chayne Thrash,Haoran Qin,Hamed Pirsiavash,Soheil Kolouri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出IO-SVD，一种基于输入-输出白化空间的SVD后训练压缩方法，通过KL散度感知的双侧白化空间、异质秩分配和损失感知的量化映射，在显著降低模型大小和推理延迟的同时最小化性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在资源受限和延迟敏感场景下部署面临存储和计算成本高的主要障碍，现有SVD后训练压缩方法存在依赖仅输入白化空间、同质秩分配或损失不可知的分配启发式等局限，限制了激进压缩下模型质量的保持。

Method: IO-SVD包含三个关键技术：1) 基于KL散度二阶展开构建输出侧度量以捕获预测敏感性，结合输入白化统计形成KL感知的双侧白化空间；2) 使用一阶校准损失估计对白化奇异分量评分，在全局预算下剪枝最不敏感分量的异质秩分配策略；3) 基于预测损失变化选择低秩因子行进行8位量化的损失感知映射，用于混合SVD-量化压缩。

Result: 在多种LLM和VLM家族上的广泛实验以及推理时间分析表明，IO-SVD以最小的性能退化压缩大语言模型，同时实现实际的推理加速。

Conclusion: IO-SVD通过损失感知的双侧白化空间和异质秩分配，显著优于现有SVD压缩方法，且可与量化结合实现混合压缩，为资源受限场景下的大模型部署提供有效方案。

Abstract: Large language models deliver strong performance across language and reasoning tasks, but their storage and compute costs remain major barriers to deployment in resource-constrained and latency-sensitive settings. SVD-based post-training compression offers a hardware-agnostic way to reduce model size and improve inference efficiency through low-rank factorization. However, existing methods often rely on input-only whitening spaces, homogeneous rank allocation, or loss-agnostic allocation heuristics, limiting their ability to preserve model quality under aggressive compression. We propose Input-Output Whitened SVD (IO-SVD), a post-training compression method that forms a KL-aware double-sided whitening space for model weights. Using a second-order expansion of the KL loss over the top-K token probabilities, IO-SVD constructs an output-side metric that captures predictive sensitivity, while input whitening captures activation statistics. We further introduce an efficient heterogeneous rank-allocation strategy that scores whitened singular components using first-order calibration loss estimates and prunes the least sensitive components under a global budget. Inspired by prior work that combines SVD truncation with quantization, we improve hybrid SVD-quantization compression through loss-aware remapping, which selects low-rank factor rows for 8-bit quantization based on the predicted loss change incurred by quantizing them. Extensive experiments across diverse LLM and VLM families, and inference-time analysis shows that IO-SVD compresses LLMs with minimal performance degradation while delivering practical inference speedups. Code is available at https://github.com/mint-vu/IO-SVD.git

</details>


### [143] [Perforated Neural Networks for Keyword Spotting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15647)
*Vishy Gopal,Aris Ilias Goutis,Ralph Crewe,Erin Yanacek,Rorry Brenner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将穿孔反向传播（Perforated Backpropagation）应用于边缘设备上的关键词识别任务，通过在标准CNN中引入人工树突节点，在800次超参数实验中发现树突模型在每种参数规模和精度阈值下均优于传统架构。最佳树突模型仅用1,500个参数即达到0.933测试精度，而基线模型需约4,000个参数才能达到0.921精度，实现了模型质量与部署效率的同时提升。该工作在Edge Impulse 2025黑客马拉松中获得最佳模型奖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘机器学习面临严格的内存预算、有限计算资源和不可妥协的精度要求，现有压缩技术通常只能在不同资源间进行权衡，难以同时提升精度和减小模型尺寸。因此需要一种能同时改善模型质量和部署效率的方法。

Method: 在Edge Impulse平台上，对标准CNN进行改进，添加人工树突节点（Dendrite Nodes），应用穿孔反向传播（Perforated Backpropagation）训练关键词识别模型。通过800次超参数实验进行系统性对比评估。

Result: 树突模型在所有参数规模和精度阈值下均优于传统架构。最佳树突模型：测试精度0.933，仅需1,500个参数；基线模型：测试精度0.921，需约4,000个参数。

Conclusion: 穿孔反向传播是边缘AI工程师工具箱中的有力补充，能够同时提升模型质量和部署效率，为边缘机器学习中的资源约束问题提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Edge machine learning presents a unique set of constraints not encountered in cloud-scale model deployment: strict memory budgets, limited compute, and non-negotiable accuracy thresholds must all be satisfied simultaneously. Existing compression and optimization techniques can trade one resource for another, but rarely improve both accuracy and model size at the same time. This paper presents the application of Perforated Backpropagation to keyword spotting on the Edge Impulse platform, an experiment that won the Best Model award at the Edge Impulse 2025 Hackathon in December 2025. By adding artificial Dendrite Nodes to a standard convolutional neural network trained on the Edge Impulse keyword spotting tutorial pipeline, we demonstrate that dendritic models outperform traditional architectures at every level of parameter count and at every accuracy threshold tested across 800 hyperparameter trials. The best dendritic model achieved a test accuracy of 0.933 with only 1,500 parameters, versus the baseline accuracy of 0.921 requiring approximately 4,000 parameters. These results suggest that Perforated Backpropagation is a powerful addition to the edge AI engineer's toolkit, offering simultaneous gains in both model quality and deployment efficiency.

</details>


### [144] [Sharp Spectral Thresholds for Logit Fixed Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15651)
*Tongxi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现并修正了Softmax反馈系统稳定性判据的经典结论，将欧几里得阈值从保守的过正则化条件精确提升至β‖ΠWΠ‖_{T→T}<2，填补了先前缺失的分岔前稳定区域，扩展了可认证稳定性的范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典理论在判定Softmax反馈系统稳定性时过于保守，仅在强随机化（过正则化）区域确认稳定性，未能识别真正发生定性变化的转折点，且遗漏了一个完整的稳定区间。

Method: 针对有限维仿射Logit系统，通过理论分析推导出尖锐的、与维度无关的欧几里得阈值条件β‖ΠWΠ‖_{T→T}<2，替代了此前过于保守的判据。

Result: 新阈值条件β‖ΠWΠ‖_{T→T}<2比经典条件更具精确性，填补了分岔前的缺失区域，使稳定性保障扩展至对奖励具有响应性但全局仍可预测的系统，并准确标定了模型相变发生的临界点。

Conclusion: 本文纠正了经典理论的不足，为熵正则化强化学习、Logit博弈动力学、群体选择及平均场变分更新等广泛应用的Softmax反馈系统提供了更精确的稳定性边界。

Abstract: Softmax feedback systems are a common mathematical core of entropy-regularized reinforcement learning, logit game dynamics, population choice, and mean-field variational updates. Their central stability question is simple: when does a self-reinforcing softmax system produce a unique and globally predictable outcome? Classical theory gives a conservative answer. By treating softmax as a unit-scale response, it certifies stability only in a strongly randomized regime.
  We prove that the classical approach misses an entire stable regime and does not identify the point at which the qualitative change truly occurs. For finite-dimensional affine logit systems, the sharp dimension-free Euclidean threshold is $$β\|ΠWΠ\|_{\mathcal T\to\mathcal T}<2,$$ rather than the previously used condition, which certifies stability only while the softmax system remains safely over-regularized. Our theorem fills the previously missing pre-bifurcation regime, extending stability guarantees for affine softmax feedback systems to reward-responsive yet globally predictable systems. It enlarges the certified stability boundary for these systems and identifies where the model genuinely undergoes a phase transition.

</details>


### [145] [On the Power of Adaptivity for $\varepsilon$-Best Arm Identification in Linear Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15663)
*Arnab Maiti,Yunbei Xu,Kevin Jamieson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了线性老虎机中ε-最佳臂识别的极小极大样本复杂度。首先给出了非自适应固定设计方法的下界与上界（均为O(d log(1/δ)/ε² + w(𝒳)²/ε²)），证明了超立方体、ℓ₂球等结构化动作集上自适应策略仅能带来对数级别的改进，并首次构造了一个动作集使得自适应策略相比非自适应方法有多项式级别的改进。关键工具是一个ℓ₂范数估计子程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线性老虎机中，ε-最佳臂识别是一个基础问题，但非自适应与自适应方法的样本复杂度差距尚不明确。特别是，对于结构化的动作集，自适应是否能带来实质性（多项式级别）的改进，此前未有定论。本文旨在系统刻画这种差距，并首次构造出能实现多项式改进的动作集。

Method: 1) 非自适应方法：提出固定设计方法，利用高斯宽度w(𝒳)刻画动作集复杂度，给出上下界匹配的结果。2) 自适应方法：针对超立方体、ℓ₂球、m-集、多任务多臂老虎机等结构，证明自适应仅带来对数因子改进。3) 构造分离实例：设计了一个ℓ₂范数估计子程序，基于此构造特定动作集，使得自适应算法能达到多项式级别的改进。

Result: 1) 非自适应方法的最优样本复杂度为Θ(d log(1/δ)/ε² + w(𝒳)²/ε²)。2) 在多种自然动作集上，自适应的优势仅在对数因子层面。3) 首次构造出动作集，使得自适应相比非自适应有多项式因子改进。

Conclusion: 非自适应方法在一般动作集上的样本复杂度已被精确刻画；自适应的优势在不同动作集上差异显著，存在从对数到多项式的巨大鸿沟；ℓ₂范数估计子程序是实现这种多项式分离的关键技术。

Abstract: We study the minimax sample complexity of $\varepsilon$-best arm identification in linear bandits. Given a compact action set $\mathcal{X}$ that spans $\mathbb{R}^d$ and an unknown reward vector $θ\in\mathbb{R}^d$, the goal is to output an arm $\widehat{x}\in\mathcal{X}$ such that $\langle \widehat{x},θ\rangle \ge \max_{x\in\mathcal{X}} \langle x,θ\rangle - \varepsilon$ with probability at least $1-δ$, using as few samples as possible.
  First, we present a non-adaptive fixed-design method with sample complexity $\mathcal{O}\!\left(\frac{d\log(1/δ)}{\varepsilon^2}+\frac{w(\mathcal{X})^2}{\varepsilon^2}\right)$, where $w(\mathcal{X})$ is a Gaussian width term dependent on $\mathcal{X}$, and we prove a matching lower bound $Ω\!\left(\frac{d\log(1/δ)}{\varepsilon^2}+\frac{w(\mathcal{X})^2}{\varepsilon^2}\right)$ for all non-adaptive fixed-design methods.
  We then turn to adaptive sampling. We raise an important structural question: beyond the canonical basis, are there structured action sets for which adaptivity yields only logarithmic-factor improvements over the optimal non-adaptive rate? We answer in the affirmative for several natural action sets, namely the hypercube, the $\ell_2$ ball, $m$-sets, and multi-task multi-armed bandits.
  Finally, we provide the first construction of an action set $\mathcal{X}$ for which adaptivity yields a polynomial-factor improvement over every non-adaptive algorithm. A key ingredient behind this separation is an $\ell_2$-norm estimation subroutine: we design an adaptive algorithm that uses $\mathcal{O}\!\left(\frac{d\log(1/δ)}{\varepsilon^2}\right)$ samples from the unit $\ell_2$ ball in $\mathbb{R}^d$ and outputs an estimate $\widehat r$ satisfying $|\widehat r-\|θ\|_2|\le \varepsilon$ with probability at least $1-δ$, where $θ$ is the unknown reward vector.

</details>


### [146] [Rule2DRC: Benchmarking LLM Agents for DRC Script Synthesis with Execution-Guided Test Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15669)
*Jinuk Kim,Junsoo Byun,Donghwi Hwang,Seong-Jin Park,Hyun Oh Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Rule2DRC是一个包含1,000个规则到脚本任务和13,921个评估芯片布局的大规模基准，用于评估LLM智能体生成DRC脚本的功能正确性。同时提出SplitTester测试智能体，通过执行反馈生成判别性测试用例来提升脚本选择性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将自然语言设计规则翻译为正确的DRC脚本需要大量专业劳动，现有基准存在评估集小、依赖代码相似度而非执行正确性等问题，且先前方法要么忽略执行反馈要么需要标注测试布局作为输入。

Method: 构建大规模基准Rule2DRC（1,000任务+13,921布局），提供基于DRC执行结果的评估流水线；提出SplitTester测试智能体，利用执行反馈生成判别性测试用例以区分难以区分的候选脚本，改进Best-of-N选择。

Result: 建立了大规模执行驱动的DRC脚本合成基准；SplitTester在候选脚本选择上显著提升了性能。

Conclusion: Rule2DRC为DRC脚本合成提供了更可靠的评估基准，SplitTester展示了利用执行反馈改进代码生成的潜力。

Abstract: Manufacturable chip layouts must satisfy thousands of geometry-based design rules, and design rule checking (DRC) enforces them by running executable DRC scripts on layouts. Translating natural language rules into correct DRC scripts is labor-intensive and requires specialized expertise, motivating LLM agents for DRC script synthesis and debugging. However, existing benchmarks have small evaluation sets and often evaluate scripts by code similarity rather than execution correctness, and prior machine learning-based methods either ignore execution feedback or require labeled test layouts as agent's input. To this end, we introduce Rule2DRC, a large-scale benchmark for DRC script coding agents with 1,000 rule-to-script tasks and 13,921 evaluation chip layouts for execution-based scoring. Rule2DRC provides an evaluation pipeline that measures functional correctness via DRC execution outcomes without requiring evaluation layouts as input to the agent. We also propose SplitTester, a tester agent for program selection that uses execution feedback to generate discriminative test cases and separate previously indistinguishable candidate scripts, substantially improving Best-of-N selection performance in this domain. We release the code at https://github.com/snu-mllab/Rule2DRC.

</details>


### [147] [Interaction-Aware Influence Functions for Group Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15675)
*Jaeseung Heo,Kyeongheung Yun,Youngbin Choi,Sehyun Hwang,Jungseul Ok,Dongwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了交互感知影响函数，通过二阶展开捕获训练样本间的交互作用对目标函数的影响，在留组估计和大模型指令微调数据选择任务上显著优于传统一阶影响函数方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统影响函数在估计一组样本的联合影响时，仅对个体影响求和，无法捕捉样本间的冗余性或互补性等交互效应，导致估计不准确。

Method: 将目标函数在训练参数处进行二阶泰勒展开，在标准一阶影响函数求和的基础上增加一个成对交互项，该项衡量两个样本对目标函数影响的向量对齐程度。

Result: 在逻辑回归、MLP和ResNet-9的六组数据集-模型组合上，该估计器在所有设定下均显著优于一阶影响函数；在Llama-3.1-8B指令微调数据贪婪选择中，在七个下游任务中的五个上超越了基于影响函数和表征相似度的基线方法，且标准影响函数在此场景下表现不如随机选择。

Conclusion: 交互感知影响函数通过引入样本间交互项，更准确地估计了样本组的联合影响，在数据选择等实际应用中有显著优势。

Abstract: Influence functions approximate how removing a training example changes a quantity of interest, called the target function, such as a held-out loss. To estimate the influence of a group of examples, the standard practice is to sum the individual influences of its members. However, this sum does not capture how examples jointly affect the target: a pair of examples may be redundant or complementary, but the sum cannot distinguish these cases. We propose an interaction-aware influence function that characterizes how interactions between examples influence the target. By expanding the target to second order around the trained parameters, we obtain an estimator that augments the standard sum with a pairwise interaction term that captures the alignment between two examples' effects on the target. We empirically evaluate our estimator in two settings. First, on six dataset-model pairs spanning logistic regression, MLPs, and ResNet-9, our estimator tracks leave-group-out retraining substantially better than first-order influence across all settings. Second, when used as a greedy selection rule for instruction-tuning data on Llama-3.1-8B, it beats prior influence-based and representation-similarity baselines on five of seven downstream tasks, in a regime where standard influence-based selection underperforms random selection.

</details>


### [148] [SEED: Targeted Data Selection by Weighted Independent Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15691)
*Yuan Zhang,Lifeng Guo,Junwen Pan,Chang Liu,Wenzhao Zheng,Kuan Cheng,Kurt Keutzer,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SEED数据选择方法，将数据选择问题建模为加权独立集（WIS），通过节点价值校准和局部尺度归一化两个改进，在指令微调、视觉指令微调和语义分割任务上持续优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据选择方法难以同时保证样本质量与集合多样性，且面临两个实际挑战：朴素节点权重无法区分信息信号与梯度噪声；异构域分布下的边构造产生结构不平衡图，导致选择偏向稀疏区域。

Method: 将数据选择建模为相似图上的加权独立集问题；提出节点价值校准（在双边显著子空间限制影响估计以捕获任务相关信号）和局部尺度归一化（根据局部邻域密度自适应调整边阈值以缓解跨域分布偏移引起的图不平衡）。

Result: 构建了紧凑多模态数据集Honeybee-Remake-SEED-200K；在指令微调、视觉指令微调和语义分割任务上，SEED持续优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 从统一图视角出发的两个改进有效解决了数据选择中的信号区分和图结构不平衡问题，SEED是一种鲁棒且可扩展的数据选择方法。

Abstract: Data selection seeks to identify a compact yet informative subset from large-scale training corpora, balancing sample quality against collection diversity. We formulate this problem as a Weighted Independent Set (WIS) on a similarity graph, where nodes represent data samples weighted by influence, and edges connect semantically redundant pairs. This formulation naturally yields subsets that are simultaneously high-quality and diverse. However, two challenges arise in practice: naive node weights fail to distinguish informative signals from gradient noise, and edge construction under heterogeneous domain distributions produces structurally imbalanced graphs that bias selection toward sparse regions. To address these issues, we introduce two principled refinements from a unified graph perspective: (1) \textit{node value calibration} that restricts influence estimation to the bilateral salient subspace to ground node importance in task-relevant signals rather than surface-level statistics; (2) \textit{local scale normalization} that adapts edge thresholds to local neighborhood density, mitigating graph imbalance induced by cross-domain distribution shifts. Together, these components yield a robust and scalable data selection pipeline dubbed SEED. We further construct \texttt{Honeybee-Remake-SEED-200K}, a compact multimodal dataset curated by SEED. Extensive experiments show that SEED consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods on instruction tuning, visual instruction tuning, and semantic segmentation across diverse model families.

</details>


### [149] [Tighter Regret Bounds for Contextual Action-Set Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15692)
*Zijun Chen,Zihan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于回合的强化学习问题，其中奖励和转移函数固定，但每回合允许的动作集（动作上下文）不同且在回合开始时已知。MVP算法可自然扩展至该框架，对对抗性上下文取得了$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{SAH^3K\log L})$的极小化最优遗憾界，对随机上下文取得$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{SAH^3K})$的遗憾界，并进一步推导了基于间隙依赖的改进上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习研究大多假设动作集在所有回合中固定不变，但在许多实际场景中（如推荐系统、个性化医疗），每回合可用的动作集可能因环境或用户而异。本文旨在将基于回合的强化学习推广到每回合动作集可变的情景，并建立相应的理论保证。

Method: 作者将MVP（Monotonic Value Propagation）算法扩展到具有上下文动作集的设置。对于对抗性上下文（上下文任意且可能敌意），给出$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{SAH^3K\log L})$的极小化最优遗憾界；对于随机上下文（上下文服从固定分布），遗憾界简化为$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{SAH^3K})$，并进一步转化为样本复杂度$\widetilde{O}(SAH^3/\epsilon^2)$。此外，还推导了一个依赖于间隙（gap）的遗憾界，利用全局$p$-修剪正间隙下界$\Delta_{\min}^p$来刻画困难程度，当子优间隙较大时可显著优于极小化最优率。

Result: 1. 对抗性上下文：$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{SAH^3K\log L})$极小化最优遗憾界。2. 随机上下文：$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{SAH^3K})$遗憾界，对应样本复杂度$\widetilde{O}(SAH^3/\epsilon^2)$。3. 间隙依赖界：$\widetilde{O}\left( \inf_{p\in[0,1)} \left( \frac{1}{\Delta_{\min}^p} + pK\Delta_{\min}^p \right)\log K \cdot \mathrm{poly}(S,A,H) \right)$，在间隙大时显著改进。

Conclusion: MVP算法能自然适应每回合动作集变化的强化学习设定，并在对抗性和随机性两种上下文场景下均取得优异的理论保证，包括极小化最优遗憾和间隙依赖的改进上界。

Abstract: We study episodic reinforcement learning with fixed reward and transition functions, but with episode-dependent admissible action sets that are observed at the start of each episode. Performance is measured by cumulative regret against the episode-wise optimal value, $\sum_{k=1}^K [V^{*,M^k} - V^{π^k,M^k}]$, where $M^k$ represents the action context in the $k$-th episode. We show that the MVP algorithm naturally extends to this framework and enjoys strong theoretical guarantees. In particular, we establish a minimax regret bound of $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{SAH^3K\log L})$ for adversarial contexts, where $L$ denotes the number of possible contexts. This result implies a regret bound of $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{SAH^3K})$ for stochastic contexts. We further translate the stochastic regret guarantee into a sample complexity bound of $\widetilde{O}(SAH^3/ε^2)$ for a fixed context distribution.
  In addition, we derive a gap-dependent regret bound of \[ \widetilde O\left( \inf_{p\in [0,1)} \left( \frac{1}{Δ_{\min}^{p}} + pKΔ_{\min}^{p} \right)\log K \cdot \mathrm{poly}(S,A,H) \right), \] where $Δ_{\min}^{p}$ is the global $p$-trimmed positive-gap floor over suboptimal $(h,s,a)$ triples. This bound can substantially improve upon the minimax rate when the relevant suboptimality gaps are large.

</details>


### [150] [Going Beyond the Edge: Distributed Inference of Transformer Models on Ultra-Low-Power Wireless Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15694)
*Alexander Gräfe,Ding Huo,Johannes Berger,Marco Zimmerling,Sebastian Trimpe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CATS是一个面向超低功耗无线设备的分布式Transformer推理框架，通过通信感知的模型分区、SomeGather剪枝通信原语和消息丢弃训练，使得多达16个设备能够协同执行单个设备无法承载的Transformer模型，模型规模可达单设备容量的14倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型在IoT应用中日益重要，但其计算和内存需求远超单个超低功耗无线设备的能力，迫切需要一种分布式协作方案来突破单设备资源限制。

Method: CATS包含三个核心设计：(1) SomeGather——一种选择性广播激活列的剪枝通信原语，降低通信带宽和RAM占用；(2) 基于SomeGather的模型分区方法，实现高效模型并行；(3) 消息丢弃训练（message-dropout），模拟无线通信中的丢包，增强模型对消息丢失的鲁棒性。

Result: 在真实世界实验中，CATS首次实现了超低功耗无线设备上的分布式Transformer推理，支持多达16台设备协同执行，模型规模可达单设备容量的14倍。

Conclusion: CATS通过通信感知的分布式推理方案，成功将Transformer推理能力扩展到超低功耗无线设备集群，验证了通过软硬件协同设计突破IoT设备资源瓶颈的可行性。

Abstract: Transformer models are rapidly becoming a cornerstone of modern Internet of Things (IoT) applications, yet their computational and memory demands far exceed the capabilities of a single typical ultra-low-power IoT device. We present CATS, a framework for distributed transformer inference on ultra-low-power wireless devices, enabling multiple devices to collaboratively execute models far larger than what a single device can sustain. At its core, CATS is a communication-aware distributed transformer inference scheme co-designed across transformer partitioning, wireless communication and training. It employs SomeGather, a new pruned communication primitive that selectively broadcasts activation columns to reduce communication bandwidth and RAM usage without sacrificing model accuracy. Building on SomeGather, we design a partitioning method that exploits this primitive for efficient model parallelism. To cope with unreliable wireless communication, CATS employs message-dropout during training, which mimics packet losses and yields models that are robust to message loss during inference. In real-world experiments, we show that CATS brings distributed transformer inference to ultra-low-power wireless devices for the first time, with deployments on up to 16 devices that collaboratively execute transformer models up to 14 times larger than what a single device can run.

</details>
