### [151] [AGOP-IxG: A Gradient Covariance Filter for Local Feature Attribution on Tabular Data, with a Controlled Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15700)
*Raj Kiran Gupta Katakam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种快速逐样本特征归因方法AGOP-IxG，通过预乘梯度与截断的AGOP矩阵实现。在三个合成表格任务上，AGOP-IxG在Spearman秩相关和噪声特征质量上领先于SHAP、Integrated Gradients等方法，且速度比SHAP快350-1650倍。在真实数据集上，各方法的全局忠实度表现相近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征归因方法（如SHAP、IG、LIME）往往基于惯例而非实际保真度选择，因为真实数据上缺乏归因的真实标签（ground-truth）。AutoML生成的模型需要可解释性，但缺乏严格评估归因方法准确性的基准。

Method: 提出AGOP-IxG方法，通过预乘每个样本的梯度与一个top-K秩截断的平均梯度外积（AGOP）矩阵。构建三个合成多分类表格任务（线性、稀疏非线性、交互基），其中每个样本的真实归因可解析或数值推导得出。在Part 1中比较AGOP-IxG与SHAP、Integrated Gradients、InputXGradient、LIME；在Part 2中使用ROAR协议评估全局忠实度。

Result: Part 1: AGOP-IxG在所有三个合成数据集上的Spearman秩相关和噪声特征质量指标领先，在交互数据集上的top-k精确率也最优；速度比SHAP快约350-1650倍。Part 2: 在Adult Income和Credit Card Default数据集上，各方法的全局忠实度（ROAR协议下的相对AUC）差异在约1.7%以内。

Conclusion: AGOP-IxG在逐样本局部归因任务上准确且高效，特别适合对速度敏感的AutoML场景；但在全局特征排名任务上，各方法表现相近，AGOP-IxG并非为此优化设计。

Abstract: Automated machine learning pipelines increasingly produce models whose predictions must be explained to end users, auditors, and downstream decision systems. The most widely used feature attribution methods (SHAP, Integrated Gradients, LIME) are typically chosen by convention rather than measured fidelity, because rigorous evaluation is impeded by the absence of ground-truth attribution on real data. We propose AGOP-IxG, a fast per-sample attribution method for tabular classifiers that pre-multiplies the per-sample gradient by a top-$K$ rank-truncated Average Gradient Outer Product matrix, and evaluate it against four widely-used baselines on a controlled tabular benchmark designed for AutoML practitioners. In Part 1, we construct three synthetic multi-class tabular tasks (linear, sparse nonlinear, interaction-based) where ground-truth attribution per sample is analytically or numerically derivable, and compare five methods: AGOP-IxG, SHAP (DeepExplainer), Integrated Gradients, InputXGradient, and LIME. AGOP-IxG leads on Spearman rank correlation and noise feature mass on all three synthetic datasets, and on top-$k$ precision on the interaction dataset. Across all settings, AGOP-IxG is approximately $350\times$ to $1{,}650\times$ faster than SHAP. In Part 2, we evaluate global faithfulness on Adult Income and Credit Card Default using the ROAR protocol; the methods cluster within $\sim 1.7\%$ relative AUC, consistent with AGOP-IxG being optimized for per-sample local attribution rather than global feature ranking.

</details>


### [152] [Differentiable Mixture-of-Agents Incentivizes Swarm Intelligence of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15706)
*Xingjian Wu,Junkai Lu,Siyu Yan,Xiangfei Qiu,Jilin Hu,Chenjuan Guo,Bin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可微混合智能体框架(DMoA)，通过可微上下文感知路由机制和预测熵自监督信号，实现推理时的动态智能体协作与自适应，在9个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAS依赖预定义或预编译的通信拓扑，缺乏灵活性和对动态任务需求的适应性。

Method: 设计可微上下文感知路由机制（结合循环结构），实现逐步骤的稀疏智能体激活；引入预测熵作为自监督信号优化路由过程，实现无需外部标注的测试时自适应。

Result: 在9个基准上取得SOTA性能，同时展现强效率、鲁棒性和集成能力。

Conclusion: DMoA通过动态路由与自监督优化，使MAS从静态预定义拓扑进化为自演化的弹性协作系统。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have catalyzed the development of multi-agent systems (MAS) for complex reasoning tasks. However, existing MAS typically rely on pre-defined or pre-compiled communication topologies, which limits their flexibility and adaptability to dynamic task requirements. In this work, we propose Differentiable Mixture-of-Agents (DMoA), a self-evolving multi-agent framework that enables elastic and adaptive agent collaboration during inference. Instead of statically constructing workflows, DMoA dynamically routes and activates agents at each reasoning step, allowing the system to implicitly simulate diverse communication topologies and adapt to evolving demands. To achieve this, we design a differentiable, context-aware routing mechanism that leverages recurrent structures to incorporate historical and contextual information, producing sparse agent activations in a step-wise manner. Furthermore, we introduce predictive entropy as self-supervised signals to optimize the routing process, enabling efficient test-time adaptation without external annotations. Extensive experiments across 9 benchmarks demonstrate that DMoA achieves state-of-the-art performance while exhibiting strong efficiency, robustness, and ensembling capabilities.

</details>


### [153] [Bidirectional Fusion Guided by Cardiac Patterns for Semi-Supervised ECG Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15722)
*Jeonghwa Lim,Minje Park,Sunghoon Joo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CardioMix，一种基于双向CutMix策略和心脏模式引导的ECG分割框架，通过增强标记与未标记数据间的信息交换，有效提升半监督心电图波形分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心电图波形精准分割对心血管诊断至关重要，但标注数据稀缺严重制约深度学习模型训练。现有半监督语义分割方法主要依赖未标注数据的一致性约束，未能充分利用标注集与未标注集之间的信息交换潜力。

Method: 提出CardioMix框架，核心是心脏模式引导的双向CutMix策略：一方面用未标注数据的真实变体丰富标注集，另一方面向未标注集施加更强的监督信号；通过心脏模式引导确保所有增强样本保持生理学意义。该框架设计为即插即用模块，兼容多种半监督分割算法。

Result: 在公开多数据集基准SemiSegECG上的大量实验表明，CardioMix作为即插即用模块，在不同数据集和标注比例下，持续优于现有基于CutMix的融合策略，且兼容多种半监督分割算法。

Conclusion: 心脏模式引导的双向CutMix策略能有效提升半监督ECG分割性能，CardioMix可作为通用模块集成到现有半监督框架中。

Abstract: Accurate delineation of electrocardiogram (ECG), the segmentation of meaningful waveform features, is crucial for cardiovascular diagnostics. However, the scarcity of annotated data poses a significant challenge for training deep learning models. Conventional semi-supervised semantic segmentation (SemiSeg) methods primarily focus on consistency from unlabeled data, underutilizing the information exchange possible between labeled and unlabeled sets. To address this, we introduce CardioMix, a framework built on a bidirectional CutMix strategy guided by cardiac patterns for ECG segmentation. This approach enriches the labeled set with realistic variations from unlabeled data while simultaneously applying stronger supervisory signals to the unlabeled set, as the cardiac pattern-guided mixing ensures all augmented samples remain physiologically meaningful. Our framework is designed as a plug-and-play module, demonstrating high compatibility with various SemiSeg algorithms. Extensive experiments on SemiSegECG, a public multi-dataset benchmark for ECG delineation, demonstrate that CardioMix consistently outperforms existing CutMix-based fusion strategies across diverse datasets and labeled ratios as a plug-and-play module compatible with various SemiSeg algorithms.

</details>


### [154] [GOMA: Toward Structure-Driven Multimodal Alignment from a Graph Signal Smoothing Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15723)
*Xu Wang,Xunkai Li,Yinlin Zhu,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GOMA是一种结构驱动的后对齐框架，通过对冻结的多模态嵌入进行图优化，解决多模态属性图中视觉、文本和跨模态关系的拓扑差异与过度平滑问题，在7个基准上实现SOTA检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CLIP式双编码器仅从孤立的图文对学习多模态对齐，忽略了实体间的关联上下文（即图结构信息）。多模态属性图（MAG）虽能提供这种结构上下文，但直接使用图传播会导致三个挑战：不同模态的邻域几何不一致、无限制传播导致表示过度平滑、难以在语义边界崩溃前保留有用的平滑信息。

Method: GOMA将冻结的多模态嵌入视为图信号，解耦三个关键设计：1) 消息流向：学习模态感知的传播算子，打破模态特定的拓扑障碍；2) 多模态证据传播：执行有限步耦合平滑，避免对角跨模态捷径（即不使用自对边）；3) 平滑深度保留：自适应读出节点特定的平滑轨迹，在语义崩溃前保留有用平滑信息。采用归纳式MAG检索协议，图仅作为无标签上下文，移除对角线自对边。

Result: 在7个MAG基准上，GOMA达到SOTA或并列SOTA的检索性能，且相比最强图基线方法具有显著更高的稳定性。

Conclusion: MAG结构可以作为冻结多模态嵌入的有效后编码器，通过结构驱动的图优化显著提升跨模态检索性能。

Abstract: Multimodal alignment is commonly learned from isolated image-text pairs via CLIP-style dual encoders, leaving the relational context among entities largely unused. Multimodal attributed graphs (MAGs), where nodes carry multimodal attributes and edges encode corpus structure, provide a natural setting for refining frozen vision-language embeddings. This refinement is challenging: visual, textual, and cross-modal relations often induce different neighborhood geometries, while unrestricted graph propagation can quickly over-smooth retrieval representations. Effectively leveraging graph context therefore requires simultaneously breaking modality-specific topological barriers, controlling the smoothing regime, and preserving informative smoothing before semantic boundaries collapse. We propose Graph-Optimized Multimodal Alignment (GOMA), a structure-driven post-alignment framework that views frozen multimodal embeddings as graph signals and addresses these requirements through a unified retrieval-oriented design. GOMA decouples three key design choices: where messages should flow, how multimodal evidence should propagate, and which smoothing depth should be retained. Concretely, it learns modality-aware propagation operators, performs finite-step coupled smoothing without diagonal cross-modal shortcuts, and adaptively reads out node-specific smoothing trajectories to preserve useful smoothing before collapse. All experiments follow a transductive MAG retrieval protocol where the graph serves only as unlabeled context and diagonal self-pair edges are removed. On seven MAG benchmarks, GOMA achieves state-of-the-art or tied state-of-the-art retrieval and remains substantially more stable than the strongest graph competitor, demonstrating that MAG structure can serve as an effective post-encoder for frozen multimodal embeddings.

</details>


### [155] [A Unified Perturbation Framework for Analyzing Leaderboard Stability and Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15761)
*Hosna Oyarhoseini,Jimmy Lin,Amir-Hossein Karimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于影响力的统一扰动框架，用于分析Bradley-Terry排行榜（如LMArena）在结构化数据修改下的鲁棒性。研究发现，现代排行榜在三个目标（top-k成员、全局排名一致性、置信区间）上均非鲁棒：低于1%的定向扰动即可改变排名、降低Kendall tau并改变置信区间。该框架还利用影响力分数实现高效定向扰动，可促进或降级特定模型，并减少不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估排行榜（如LMArena）在聚合人类偏好进行模型排名方面发挥核心作用，但其排名鲁棒性尚未被充分理解。现有研究缺乏系统性的框架来量化排行榜在数据扰动下的稳定性。

Method: 采用基于影响力的近似方法，构建统一扰动框架。研究三种匹配级扰动（Drop、Add、Flip）及玩家移除，通过top-k成员、Kendall's tau和置信区间三个目标评估影响。在Chatbot Arena及六个额外成对比较数据集上进行实验。

Result: 现代排行榜在三个评估目标上均非鲁棒：低于1%的定向扰动即可改变榜首模型、降低Kendall's tau并改变置信区间。影响力分数还可实现高效的定向扰动，在促进/降级特定模型和减少不确定性方面优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 该框架通过归一化的数据集级鲁棒性分数，为审计排行榜稳定性提供了实用工具，并激励开发更鲁棒的评估协议。

Abstract: Evaluation leaderboards such as LMArena play a central role in benchmarking large language models by aggregating pairwise human preferences into model rankings, yet the robustness of these rankings remains poorly understood. We present a unified perturbation framework for analyzing Bradley-Terry leaderboards under structured data modifications using influence-based approximations. Our framework studies three match-level perturbations -- Drop, Add, and Flip -- together with player removal, and evaluates their effects on top-k membership, global ranking consistency via Kendall's tau, and confidence-interval-based uncertainty. Across Chatbot Arena and six additional pairwise-comparison datasets, we show that modern leaderboards are non-robust across all three objectives: sub-1% targeted perturbations can change the top-ranked model, degrade Kendall's tau, and alter confidence intervals. Beyond robustness auditing, we show that the same influence scores enable efficient targeted perturbations, promoting or demoting specific models and reducing target-model uncertainty with fewer actions than previous manipulation and active-sampling baselines. By summarizing these effects with normalized dataset-level robustness scores, our framework provides a practical and helpful tool for auditing leaderboard stability and motivating more robust evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [156] [Continual Learning of Domain-Invariant Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15775)
*Pascal Janetzky,Tobias Schlagenhauf,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种持续学习中的域不变表示学习方法，通过结合重放训练和序列不变对齐，解决了现有CL方法因学习领域特定线索而导致泛化能力差的问题，在多个基准数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法优化域内性能，容易学习到领域特定的虚假线索（捷径学习），导致部署到未见过的目标域时泛化能力受限。

Method: 提出结合重放训练与序列不变对齐的方法，在持续学习过程中逐步学习并保留跨领域的域不变表示，利用因果机制中的不变结构来减少对领域特定线索的过拟合。

Result: 在涵盖视觉、医学、制造和生态学等领域的六个基准数据集上，所提方法在未见目标域的泛化性能上持续优于现有CL基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文首次将域不变表示学习方法引入持续学习领域；消融实验表明，简单地将现有DIRL方法扩展到序列训练中效果有限，而所提的序列不变对齐策略更为有效。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) aims to train models sequentially over multiple domains without forgetting previously learned knowledge. However, existing CL methods optimize for in-domain performance and are therefore prone to learning spurious, domain-specific cues (``shortcut learning''), which limits generalization to unseen domains after deployment. In this paper, we address this limitation through continual learning of domain-invariant representation. We introduce a broad class of CL methods that sequentially learn representations capturing invariant structures across domains. Our methods are motivated by the observation that such invariant structures often preserve the underlying causal mechanisms, which can reduce the risk of overfitting to domain-specific cues and thus offer better out-of-domain generalization. Our proposed CL methods combine replay-based training with a tailored sequential invariance alignment to learn -- and preserve -- invariant structures over time. We evaluate our methods under a deployment-oriented protocol that measures performance on unseen target domains. Across six benchmark and real-world datasets spanning vision, medicine, manufacturing, and ecology, our methods consistently outperform existing CL baselines in terms of generalization to unseen target domains. As an ablation, we further show that naïve extensions of sequential training with existing domain-invariant representation learning (DIRL) methods provide only limited benefits. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to develop domain-invariant representation methods for CL.

</details>


### [157] [AOT-POT: Adaptive Operator Transformation for Large-Scale PDE Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15793)
*Qitan Lv,Hong Wang,Zhongkai Hao,Wen Wu,Xuenan Xu,Bowen Zhou,Feng Wu,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AOT-POT框架，通过自适应算子变换将复杂多样的PDE解算子统一为更易建模的形式，仅增加3%参数就在12个PDE基准上平均降低40.9%的L2误差，微调后内外域误差分别降低92%和89%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多PDE预训练方法主要通过增加模型容量来应对解算子的复杂性和结构多样性，而忽略了目标算子本身的可建模性。受经典数值分析启发，本文旨在通过将复杂多样的解算子变换为更简单、对齐更好的形式来降低多PDE联合建模的难度。

Method: AOT-POT将隐藏表示扩展为多个并行流，在每个子层前后进行自适应的信息聚合与再分配，并通过Sinkhorn投影的双随机矩阵实现流间混合。该机制自适应地将不同PDE的解算子重塑为统一形式，从而被单一架构有效建模。

Result: 在12个PDE基准上取得SOTA结果，仅增加3%参数，相对L2误差最高降低77.6%（平均40.9%）。微调后，域内PDE误差降低92%，域外（预训练未见类型）误差降低89%。

Conclusion: 自适应算子变换是超越单纯扩展模型容量的有效且互补的方向，为构建PDE基础模型提供了新范式。

Abstract: Pre-training neural operators on diverse partial differential equation (PDE) datasets has emerged as a promising direction for building general-purpose surrogate models in scientific machine learning. However, the inherent complexity and structural diversity of PDE solution operators make multi-PDE pre-training fundamentally challenging. Existing methods mainly address this by increasing model capacity, while leaving the target solution operators unchanged. Inspired by classical numerical analysis, we instead propose to transform complex and diverse solution operators into simpler, better-aligned forms that are easier to model jointly. Since the optimal transformation varies across PDE types, it must be adaptive and input-dependent, allowing a single neural operator to approximate an entire family of operators. We instantiate this idea as AOT-POT (adaptive operator-transformation for pre-training operator transformer), which expands hidden representations into multiple parallel streams, adaptively aggregates and redistributes them before and after each sub-layer, and mixes streams through Sinkhorn-projected doubly stochastic matrices for stable training. These mechanisms together reshape diverse solution operators into a unified form that can be effectively modeled by a single architecture. Empirically, AOT-POT achieves state-of-the-art performance on 12 PDE benchmarks with only 3\% additional parameters, reducing relative L2 error by up to 77.6\% (40.9\% on average). Fine-tuning AOT-POT further reduces L2 error by up to 92\% on in-domain PDEs and 89\% on out-of-domain PDEs (unseen types during pre-training), demonstrating that adaptive operator transformation is an effective and complementary direction for advancing PDE foundation models beyond simply scaling model capacity.

</details>


### [158] [Martingale Neural Operators: Learning Stochastic Marginals via Doob-Meyer Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15806)
*Kai Hidajat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Martingale Neural Operator (MNO)，将Doob-Meyer分解定理编码为架构先验，使神经算子能够直接从初始条件预测终端分布的均值和协方差，在多种SPDE任务上将Wasserstein距离降低最高120倍，同时保持算子的一次性推理效率和分辨率不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子作为确定性替代模型时，应用于随机PDE会塌缩到条件均值，丢失方差和尾部结构信息。传统恢复这些结构的方法（蒙特卡洛 rollout 或生成模型）放弃了算子范式的一次性效率和分辨率不变性。

Method: 基于Doob-Meyer分解定理（任意半鞅可分解为可预测漂移和不可预测的零均值鞅），将其转化为架构先验。MNO将初始条件直接映射到终端分布的条件均值和协方差，其中均值类似漂移项，协方差通过低秩因子B_φ参数化，保证B_φ^⊤B_φ半正定。实验采用高斯残差实例化。

Result: 在1D SPDE、粗糙波动率和2D算子任务上：MNO将Wasserstein距离降低最高120倍（φ^4场论）和68倍（随机Burgers），评估速度比条件扩散基线快约3倍（相同壁钟训练预算）。在2D任务上，MNO在零样本分辨率迁移和湍流中与FNO相当。准确定性系统（如Gray-Scott）仍是失败模式。

Conclusion: MNO成功将Doob-Meyer定理作为架构先验，实现了神经算子在随机PDE中同时保持一次性推理效率和不确定性量化能力，在扩散型SPDE上效果显著，但在准确定性系统上仍有局限。

Abstract: Neural operators excel as deterministic surrogates, but inevitably collapse to the conditional mean when applied to stochastic PDEs, discarding the variance and tail structure upon which uncertainty quantification depends. Recovering this structure typically requires Monte Carlo rollouts or grafted generative models, both of which surrender the one-shot efficiency and resolution invariance that define the operator paradigm. To resolve this, we draw on the Doob-Meyer theorem, which establishes that any semimartingale fundamentally decomposes into a predictable drift and an unpredictable, zero-mean martingale. Translating this theorem into an architectural prior, we introduce the Martingale Neural Operator (MNO). MNO maps an initial condition directly to the conditional mean and covariance of the terminal law, parameterized by a drift-like mean and a low-rank factor $B_φ$ with $B_φ^\top B_φ$ positive semi-definite by construction. For our experiments, we use a Gaussian residual instantiation. Across 1D SPDEs, rough volatility, and 2D operator tasks, MNO reduces Wasserstein distance by up to $120\times$ on $φ^4$ field theory and $68\times$ on stochastic Burgers, evaluating $\sim 3\times$ faster than a conditional diffusion baseline at matched wall-clock training budgets. On 2D tasks, MNO is comparable to FNO on zero-shot resolution transfer and turbulent flow, while quasi-deterministic systems such as Gray-Scott remain a failure mode.

</details>


### [159] [LoCO: Low-rank Compositional Rotation Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15916)
*An Nguyen,Jaesik Choi,Anh Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoCO是一种通过低秩斜对称矩阵和组合旋转链构造正交变换的参数高效微调方法，在扩散Transformer、视觉Transformer和语言模型等任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PEFT方法（如LoRA）虽通过低秩权重更新实现参数效率，但无法保留预训练表示的几何结构，限制了模型性能。

Method: 提出LoCO方法，利用低秩斜对称矩阵构造正交变换，并通过组合旋转链实现正交性；设计近似方案实现组合旋转的全并行计算，在保持低计算复杂度的同时控制近似误差。

Result: 在扩散Transformer微调、视觉Transformer适配和语言模型适配等不同领域验证，LoCO优于或媲美现有正交及非正交方法。

Conclusion: LoCO在参数高效微调中有效保留了预训练表示的几何结构，且兼具计算效率与性能优势。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) has emerged as an critical technique for adapting large-scale foundation models across natural language processing and computer vision. While existing methods such as low-rank adaptations achieve parameter efficiency via low-rank weight updates, they are limited in their ability to preserve the geometric structure of pretrained representations. We introduce Low-rank Compositional Orthogonal fine-tuning (LoCO), a novel PEFT method that constructs orthogonal transformations through low-rank skew-symmetric matrices and compositional rotation chains. We propose an approximation scheme that enables fully parallel computation of compositional rotations, making the approach practical for high-dimensional feature spaces. Our method maintains low computational complexity while maintaining orthogonality with controlled approximation error. We validate LoCO across diverse domains, including diffusion transformer fine-tuning, vision transformer adaptation, and language model adaptation. Our method demonstrates superior or competitive performance compared to both existing orthogonal and non-orthogonal methods.

</details>


### [160] [When and Why Adversarial Training Improves PINNs: A Neural Tangent Kernel Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15959)
*Yuan-dong Cao,Chi Chiu SO,Jun-Min Wang,He Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个分析对抗训练PINNs的新框架，解释了GANs鉴别器如何影响PINNs训练动力学，提供了理论基础，并导出了更高效实用的训练算法，在多个基准上精度提升数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs存在谱偏差、刚度、高频/多尺度解精度差等训练困难，而基于GANs的对抗训练虽效果显著但其内在机制尚未被理解，缺乏理论支撑。

Method: 提出新的分析框架：(1) 从理论角度解释对抗训练在PINNs中有效的原因和条件；(2) 统一分析多种GANs变体在PINNs对抗训练中的作用；(3) 基于理论推导出新的高效训练算法。

Result: 新方法显著缓解了PINNs的训练病态问题，生成的模型在性能上优于现有方法，精度常高出数个数量级。

Conclusion: 对抗训练通过鉴别器影响PINNs训练动力学，该框架提供了理论支撑并导出了更优的实用算法。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) are powerful surrogates for differential equations but are notoriously difficult to train due to spectral bias, stiffness, and poor accuracy on high-frequency or multiscale solutions. Adversarial training based on generative adversarial networks (GANs) has recently gained surprisingly strong empirical results in improving training, but the underlying mechanisms remain elusive. To this end, we propose a new analysis framework for adversarially trained PINNs, based on the key observation of how the discriminator in GANs can influence the training dynamics of PINNs. The framework first provides a much needed theoretical grounding to why and when adversarial training is effective in PINNs, then presents a unified analysis of GANs variants in such training, and finally leads to a new, practical, efficient training algorithm for PINNs. Empirical results demonstrate that our method can significantly reduce the pathology of PINNs training, thereby providing better models with superior performances, often several magnitudes more accurate than alternative methods.

</details>


### [161] [Practical Validity Conditions for Byzantine-Tolerant Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15887)
*Mélanie Cambus,Darya Melnyk,Tijana Milentijević,Stefan Schmid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出最小包围球（MEB）有效性条件及其松弛变体c-MEB，用于拜占庭鲁棒聚合。严格MEB仍受维度限制，而松弛c-MEB（c<√2）在诚实多数（n>2t）下可实现。给出了最优MinMax-MEB规则，证明了标准聚合器的松弛MEB保证，并统一了凸、松弛凸和箱式有效性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有凸有效性条件在理论上提供强几何保证，但在现代联邦学习中存在维度依赖的鲁棒性局限，且排除了许多实用的聚合规则，亟需更实用的有效性条件。

Method: 引入MEB有效性条件及其乘性松弛c-MEB；设计MinMax-MEB聚合规则并证明其最优性（c<√2）；分析标准聚合器（最小直径平均、medoid、几何中位数）的松弛MEB保证；建立MEB与凸/松弛凸/箱式有效性的关联映射。

Result: 严格MEB有效性仍受限于维度；松弛c-MEB在n>2t时可达；MinMax-MEB达到c<√2的最优界；标准聚合器均满足松弛MEB保证；明确了各种几何有效性条件之间的系统关系。

Conclusion: 松弛MEB有效性连接了分布式计算中的有效性条件与拜占庭鲁棒聚合规则，为凸有效性提供了实用的替代方案。

Abstract: Robust aggregation is the core operation in Byzantine-tolerant federated learning. To ensure the quality of aggregation independently of data distribution or attacks, validity conditions are needed. They provide geometric guarantees of where the output of the aggregation must lie. The widespread convex validity requires the output to lie in the convex hull of the honest vectors. Although this guarantee is strong in theory, it is poorly suited to modern federated learning systems, as it has dimension-dependent resilience and excludes many practical aggregation rules.
  We introduce the minimum enclosing ball (MEB) validity condition for robust aggregation, as well as its multiplicative relaxation, $c$-MEB validity, where $c$ is a constant. We show that exact MEB validity still suffers from limited resilience, while relaxed $c$-MEB validity is achievable if a majority of clients is honest, i.e. $n>2t$. We give an optimal MinMax-MEB rule for the relaxed condition with the bound $c<\sqrt{2}$ and prove explicit relaxed-MEB guarantees for standard aggregators including minimum-diameter averaging, medoid and geometric median. Finally, we relate MEB validity to convex, relaxed-convex and box validity studied in prior literature, thus providing a systematic map of geometric validity conditions for Byzantine-robust aggregation. Our results show that relaxed MEB validity connects validity conditions in distributed computing and Byzantine-tolerant aggregation rules, and offers a practical alternative to convex validity.

</details>


### [162] [A Retrieval-Enhanced Transformer for Multi-Step Port-of-Call Sequence Prediction in Global Liner Shipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15937)
*Yanzhao Su,Fang He,Yineng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种连接约束与检索增强（CCRE）深度学习框架，用于多步港口序列预测。该框架通过检索增强历史编码器从全球海事数据库中查询相似航行先例，结合Transformer轨迹编码器进行中间融合，并采用自回归Transformer解码器进行联合序列生成，通过拓扑掩码保证航线可行性。在全球数据集上，CCRE实现了72.3%的首港准确率和61.4%的平均三步准确率，优于CatBoost和LSTM基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在预测多步港口序列时面临航线时间表不可靠、AIS数据无法提供第二港之后信息的问题，导致预测准确性不足，难以支持战术资源调度和物流效率优化。

Method: CCRE框架包含三个核心模块：(1) 检索增强历史编码器，从全球海事数据库检索相似航行先例并转化为候选级语义表示；(2) Transformer轨迹编码器，处理实时运动学数据；(3) 通过交叉注意力实现"中间融合"，动态调整短期运动学依赖与长期历史先例的权重。预测采用自回归Transformer解码器，结合Scheduled Sampling和Gumbel-Softmax松弛缓解误差累积，拓扑掩码强制满足航线可达性约束。

Result: 在全球数据集上，CCRE达到72.3%的首港准确率和61.4%的平均三步准确率，相比CatBoost和LSTM分别平均提升12.6%和11.3%。案例研究进一步验证了模型的可扩展性和对复杂航线模式的捕捉能力。

Conclusion: CCRE框架通过引入检索增强机制和序列级生成策略，有效解决了长尾航线数据稀疏和路由歧义问题，为多步港口序列预测提供了可行且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate multi-step port-of-call sequence prediction is vital for tactical resource orchestration and logistical efficiency. However, existing methods struggle with unreliable voyage schedules and the inability of AIS data to provide visibility beyond the immediate next port. To address this, this study proposes a Connectivity-Constrained and Retrieval-Enhanced (CCRE) deep learning framework. Inspired by Retrieval-Augmented Generation, CCRE introduces a retrieval-enhanced historical encoder that queries a global maritime database for contextually similar navigational precedents. Transforming these scenarios into candidate-level semantic representations compensates for data sparsity in long-tail routes and resolves routing ambiguities. Integrating this with a Transformer-based trajectory encoder, the architecture executes adaptive "middle fusion" via cross-attention. This dynamically shifts predictive reliance from real-time kinematics for short-term accuracy to historical context for long-term strategic stability. To ensure sequence-level coherence, forecasting is formulated as a joint sequence generation problem using an autoregressive Transformer decoder enriched with Scheduled Sampling and Gumbel-Softmax relaxation. This mitigates error accumulation, while topology masks strictly enforce maritime network reachability to eliminate operationally infeasible routes. Evaluated on a global dataset, CCRE achieves a 72.3% first-destination accuracy and a 61.4% average three-step accuracy, outperforming baselines like CatBoost and LSTM by average margins of 12.6% and 11.3%, respectively. Case studies further corroborate the model's scalability and ability to capture complex routing patterns across diverse international trade lanes.

</details>


### [163] [Ada-Diffuser: Latent-Aware Adaptive Diffusion for Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16054)
*Fan Feng,Selena Ge,Minghao Fu,Zijian Li,Yujia Zheng,Zeyu Tang,Yingyao Hu,Biwei Huang,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Ada-Diffuser框架，通过因果扩散模型同时学习观测交互的时序结构和潜在动态，实现在最小充分观测下对潜在过程的可识别推断，并用于规划与控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于生成模型（如扩散模型）的决策方法忽略了具有演化动态的潜在因素，而这些因素对环境转移、奖励结构和智能体高层行为至关重要。显式建模隐藏过程对精确动力学建模和有效决策不可或缺。

Method: 提出Ada-Diffuser框架：1) 理论上证明在温和条件下可从短时间窗口的观测中识别潜在过程；2) 构建因果扩散模型，同时学习观测交互的时序结构和潜在动态；3) 采用模块化设计，支持规划与策略学习两种任务，适应动态、奖励和潜在动作的变化。

Result: 在模拟控制和机器人基准实验上验证了方法的有效性，实现了准确的潜在推断和自适应策略学习。

Conclusion: 显式建模潜在动态并将其融入生成式决策框架是可行的，通过可识别性理论保证和模块化架构设计，Ada-Diffuser能够有效提升在动态环境中的决策性能。

Abstract: Recent work has framed decision-making as a sequence modeling problem using generative models such as diffusion models. Although promising, these approaches often overlook latent factors that exhibit evolving dynamics, elements that are fundamental to environment transitions, reward structures, and high-level agent behavior. Explicitly modeling these hidden processes is essential for both precise dynamics modeling and effective decision-making. In this paper, we propose a unified framework that explicitly incorporates latent dynamic inference into generative decision-making from minimal yet sufficient observations. We theoretically show that under mild conditions, the latent process can be identified from small temporal blocks of observations. Building on this insight, we introduce Ada-Diffuser, a causal diffusion model that learns the temporal structure of observed interactions and the underlying latent dynamics simultaneously, and furthermore, leverages them for planning and control. With a modular design, Ada-Diffuser supports both planning and policy learning tasks, enabling adaptation to latent variations in dynamics, rewards, and latent actions. Experiments on simulated control and robotic benchmarks demonstrate its effectiveness in accurate latent inference and adaptive policy learning.

</details>


### [164] [Entropy-Based Characterisation of the Polarised Regime in Latent Variable Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15965)
*Peter Clapham,Lisa Bonheme,Marek Grzes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于信息论的熵准则来分类VAE中的极化现象（主动/被动/混合隐变量），理论证明熵与KL散度的耦合关系，并揭示均值熵无法单独区分主动与混合维度。实验验证该方法在β-VAE、可识别VAE等多种模型中的有效性，并发现被动维度在适当归一化后仍可提升下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VAE极化现象中识别主动维度的方法依赖于高斯先验，限制了其适用范围。需要一种更通用的信息论方法来分类极化状态。

Method: 提出基于均值表示熵的信息论分类准则，通过熵-方差界理论证明熵与KL最小化的耦合关系，并分析了该方法与Bonheme主/被动条件的关联。使用均值熵和方差表示联合识别极化状态。

Result: 在β-VAE、可识别VAE、最小体积自编码器、L2正则化自编码器上验证，熵准则一致地恢复了极化现象。被动维度在适当归一化后仍可对下游任务提供小幅但一致的改进。

Conclusion: 均值熵可有效检测极化现象，但单独使用无法区分主动与混合维度，仍需方差信号的辅助。隐变量崩溃更多是尺度问题而非绝对的信息移除。

Abstract: Variational Autoencoders (VAEs) often exhibit a polarised regime in which latent variables separate into active, passive, and mixed subsets. Existing criteria for identifying active dimensions depend on a Gaussian prior, limiting their applicability to variational models and specific priors. We propose a simple information-theoretic classification of the polarised regime based on the entropy of the mean representation. We show theoretically how this entropy couples to KL minimisation through entropy--variance bounds, and we relate the resulting criterion to Bonheme's active/passive conditions. We also clarify a key limitation: entropy of the mean alone cannot reliably distinguish active from mixed dimensions without additional signals from the variance representation. Empirically, we evaluate the entropy criterion on $β$-VAEs, identifiable VAEs, Least-Volume Autoencoders, and L2-regularised autoencoders, and find that it consistently recovers a polarised regime when such a regime is present across the model classes studied. Finally, we show that passive dimensions can yield small but consistent improvements on downstream tasks when latent codes are appropriately normalised, suggesting that collapse is often a matter of scale rather than absolute information removal.

</details>


### [165] [Multi-level Self-supervised Pretraining on Compositional Hierarchical Graph for Molecular Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16088)
*Xiayu Liu,Zhengyi Lu,Hou-biao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MolCHG是一个基于组合层次图的分子图多级自监督预训练框架，将分子结构组织为跨三个语义层次的四种节点类型，引入与原子图并行的键图，使键级信息独立演化，并设计了三个层级特定的预训练目标。在MoleculeNet九个基准测试中，七个数据集上取得最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子图自监督预训练方法大多在单一结构粒度上操作，且将键信息仅作为辅助边属性而非独立语义层，未能充分利用多级结构信息。

Method: 提出组合层次图（Compositional Hierarchical Graph），包含原子级（原子节点）、键级（键节点）、片段级（片段节点）和图级（全局节点）四个节点类型。引入与原子图并行的独立键图，使键信息演化为独立节点表示。设计三个层级预训练任务：原子-键跨视图对比学习、片段级官能团预测、图级结构预测。

Result: 在MoleculeNet九个基准测试中，MolCHG在七个数据集（涵盖分类和回归任务）上取得最佳性能，其余数据集上与最强基线持平。消融实验验证多级监督信号互补，各组件均有贡献。

Conclusion: 将键信息提升为独立语义层的多级层次图结构能有效增强分子表示学习，多级预训练任务相互补充，共同提升分子性质预测性能。

Abstract: Self-supervised pretraining on molecular graphs has emerged as a promising approach for molecular property prediction, yet most existing methods operate at a single structural granularity and treat bond information as auxiliary edge attributes rather than as an independent semantic layer. In this work, we propose MolCHG, a multi-level self-supervised pretraining framework built upon a novel Compositional Hierarchical Graph that organizes molecular structure into four types of nodes across three semantic levels. By introducing a bond graph that operates in parallel with the atom graph, our architecture elevates bond-level information to independently evolving node representations, enabling fragment nodes to aggregate atom-level and bond-level semantics on an equal footing. We design three level-specific pretraining objectives: an atom-bond cross-view contrastive task that aligns the atom-view and bond-view representations within each fragment, a fragment-level functional group prediction task to inject domain-relevant chemical knowledge, and graph-level structure prediction tasks to encode global molecular topology. Experiments on nine MoleculeNet benchmarks demonstrate that MolCHG achieves the best performance on seven datasets across both classification and regression tasks, remaining competitive with the strongest baselines on the rest. Ablation studies further confirm that the multi-level supervision signals are complementary and that each component contributes to the overall performance.

</details>


### [166] [Centralized vs Decentralized Federated Learning: A trade-off performance analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16089)
*Chaimaa Medjadji,Guilain Leduc,Sylvain Kubler,Yves Le Traon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过Fedstellar模拟器、MNIST数据集和MLP分类器，实验比较了集中式、去中心化和半去中心化三种联邦学习架构的性能权衡与优劣。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对集中式、去中心化和半去中心化三种联邦学习架构的实验比较，无法为应用场景选择合适架构提供依据。

Method: 使用Fedstellar模拟器，采用MNIST数据集和MLP分类器，对CFL、DFL和SDFL三种架构进行实验分析。

Result: 实验揭示了三种FL架构在不同性能指标上的权衡关系与各自的优缺点。

Conclusion: 为不同应用需求下的FL架构选择提供了实验依据，填补了三种架构系统比较的研究空白。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for collaborative model training across distributed edge devices while preserving data privacy especially with the huge increase amount of data due to the adoption of technologies which contributes to the growing number of IoT devices. Storing this amount of data centrally is challenging due to issues like limited communication, privacy, and regulations. FL can be Centralized (CFL), Decentralized (DFL), and Semi-decentralized (SDFL). Choosing the right FL architecture depends on the application's needs. However, very few research studies have experimentally compared these three types of architectures to not only understand the respective strengths and limitations, but also trade-offs between different performance indicators. This paper overcome this lack of analysis, conducting experimental analyses using the Fedstellar simulator, MNIST dataset, and MLP classifier.

</details>


### [167] [Accelerated Gradient Descent for Faster Convergence with Minimal Overhead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16017)
*Manuel Graca,L. Miguel Silveira,Arlindo Oliveira,Frank Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CT-AGD（曲率调谐加速梯度下降），一种通过有限差分捕捉局部曲率来加速一阶优化方法的通用提升过程，在保持与Adam相当的计算开销下，平均减少33%的训练轮次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中非凸优化问题的训练效率有待提升，现有的一阶方法收敛速度较慢，而二阶方法计算开销过大。

Method: 提出CT-AGD，利用有限差分商显式捕捉局部曲率信息，并开发启发式策略来缓解随机小批量训练引入的噪声和偏差，从而加速一阶优化方法。

Result: 在保持与基线一阶方法相同精度的前提下，平均减少33%的训练轮次，且存储和计算开销与Adam等自适应梯度方法相当。

Conclusion: CT-AGD是一种高效且实用的非凸优化加速方法，在计算开销可接受的情况下显著提升训练效率。

Abstract: In this paper, we present CT-AGD (Curvature-Tuned Accelerated Gradient Descent), an optimization method for non-convex optimization problems in deep learning training tasks. CT-AGD is a general boosting procedure that accelerates first-order methods by explicitly capturing the local curvature using finite-difference quotients, and the development of heuristics aimed at mitigating noise and bias introduced by stochastic mini-batch training. CT-AGD has a comparable storage and computational overhead as adaptive gradient methods such as Adam. Our extensive experiments demonstrate that CT-AGD achieves the same level of accuracy as the baseline first-order methods, yet reduces the required training epochs by 33% on average.

</details>


### [168] [Federated Imputation under Heterogeneous Feature Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16099)
*Imane Hocine,Chaimaa Medjadji,Sylvain Kubler,Gregoire Danoy,Yves Le Traon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedHF-Impute框架，通过共享全局特征图和消息传递机制，解决联邦学习中客户端特征模式部分重叠时的数据插补问题，在SECOM和AirQuality数据集上分别提升26.9%和8.4%的插补精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习方法假设客户端特征模式对齐，但在表格数据场景中客户端通常仅观测到部分重叠的特征子集，参数平均方法（如FedAvg）在弱重叠或不相交特征组间信息传递有限，无法有效进行联邦插补。

Method: 将结构性特征不可用与传统缺失值区分开，构建共享全局特征图，通过消息传递在统计相关的特征间传播信息，实现跨客户端间接知识迁移，同时保持标准联邦通信协议。

Result: 在SECOM数据集上相比FL基线提升26.9%，AirQuality数据集提升8.4%，PhysioNET上仅与最优基线相差0.3%。

Conclusion: FedHF-Impute能够有效处理特征模式部分重叠场景下的联邦插补问题，通过特征图消息传递实现跨客户端知识迁移，显著提升插补精度。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative training across decentralized clients, but most methods assume aligned feature schemas, an assumption that rarely holds in tabular settings where clients observe only partially overlapping feature subsets. In these heterogeneous feature spaces, parameter-averaging methods (e.g., FedAvg) transfer little information across weakly overlapping or disjoint feature groups, limiting their effectiveness for federated imputation. To overcome this, we propose \textbf{FedHF-Impute}, a federated imputation framework that separates structural feature unavailability from conventional missingness and uses a shared global feature graph to propagate information across statistically related features through message passing. This enables indirect cross-client knowledge transfer, even when features are never jointly observed locally, while preserving standard federated communication. Under simulated partial schema overlap on the SECOM and AirQuality datasets, FedHF-Impute improves imputation accuracy (RMSE) over FL baselines by 26.9\%, and 8.4\% respectively, while achieving comparable performance on PhysioNET, with only a 0.3\% difference relative to the best baseline.

</details>


### [169] [Attention Dispersion in Dynamic Graph Transformers: Diagnosis and a Transferable Fix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16112)
*Jinhao Zhang,Kangfei Zhao,Qiuhao Zeng,Long-Kai Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现动态图Transformer在时间分布偏移下存在注意力分散问题，提出用差分注意力替代标准注意力，通过抑制共模信号、增强关键节点区分度来提升性能。基于此设计的DiffDyG模型在9个基准数据集上达到SOTA，尤其在高偏移场景下提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的连续时间动态图学习方法在时间分布偏移场景下性能受限，作者识别出注意力分散（attention dispersion）是共享的失败模式，并试图找到解决方案。

Method: 通过控制消融实验对比结构/时间上显著的历史邻居与随机邻居，证明预测依赖于一类携带更强预测信号的关键节点；提出用差分注意力（differential attention）替代标准自注意力，抑制共模注意力、增强令牌级差异信号。

Result: 差分注意力在三种代表性CTDG Transformer基线上一致提升性能，增益集中在高偏移数据集；DiffDyG在9个基准和三种负采样协议下达到SOTA，高偏移场景提升最大；注意力熵降低、关键节点注意力质量提升。

Conclusion: 注意力分散是CTDG Transformer在时间分布偏移下的核心问题，差分注意力是一种简单可迁移的修复方案，有效提升模型在分布偏移下的鲁棒性和预测性能。

Abstract: Transformer-based architectures have become the dominant paradigm for Continuous-Time Dynamic Graph (CTDG) learning, yet their performance remains limited on temporally shifted datasets. In this work, we identify attention dispersion as a shared failure mode of dynamic graph Transformers under temporal distribution shift. Through controlled ablation contrasting structurally and temporally distinguished historical neighbors against random ones, we show that prediction depends on a class of critical nodes that carry consistently more predictive signal than arbitrary neighbors. However, existing Transformers fail to focus on these nodes even when they are present in the input, as temporal shift weakens attention contrast and produces overly dispersed attention distributions. This diagnosis suggests a simple and transferable fix: replace standard attention with differential attention, which suppresses common-mode attention and amplifies distinctive token-level signals. When added to three representative CTDG Transformer baselines, differential attention consistently improves performance, with gains concentrated on high-shift datasets. Attention-level measurements further confirm the mechanism, showing reduced attention entropy and increased attention mass on critical nodes. Building on these findings, we introduce DiffDyG, a reference implementation combining differential attention with standard input encodings. Across 9 benchmarks and three negative sampling protocols, DiffDyG achieves SOTA performance, with especially large gains on the most shifted datasets.

</details>


### [170] [Mind Dreamer: Untethering Imagination via Active Latent Intervention on Latent Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16030)
*Shaojun Xu,Xiaoling Zhou,Yihan Lin,Yapeng Meng,Xinglong Ji,Luping Shi,Rong Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Mind Dreamer (MD)通过主动潜变量干预（ALI）框架，利用生成器采样非连续的初始潜状态，打破历史锚定效应，再通过中继值函数（RVF）和中继不确定性函数（RUF）在空间断裂处传播奖励与不确定性，实现了在稀疏奖励任务中最高8.8倍、平均1.67倍于DreamerV3的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的强化学习虽具备潜在想象能力，但其想象初始化依赖于观测状态，造成历史锚定（Historical Tethering）不对称性：世界模型流形发现速度快于策略在稀疏奖励下的优化，导致策略陷入局部盲区。

Method: 提出Mind Dreamer框架，核心包括：1）主动潜变量干预（ALI），用对抗生成器从$s_0 \sim p_{gen}(\cdot)$采样初始状态，而非经验回放缓冲区，生成物理可行但认知困难的非连续潜跳跃；2）推导中继价值函数（RVF）和中继不确定性函数（RUF），将合成锚点视为反事实中介状态，通过Bellman形式传播实用与认知价值；3）证明不连续空间中的不确定性传播需二次折扣$\gamma^2$，建立形式化认知视界。

Result: 在DeepMind Control Suite上，MD相比DreamerV3平均加速1.67倍，稀疏奖励任务中最高达8.8倍。

Conclusion: MD通过主动生成认知挑战性初始状态并设计对应的价值与不确定性传播机制，有效扩大了流形谱间隙，降低了到达关键瓶颈状态的命中时间，在理论和实验层面均验证了打破历史锚定对MBRL稀疏奖励场景的有效性。

Abstract: Model-Based Reinforcement Learning (MBRL) leverages latent imagination for sample efficiency, yet remains constrained by Historical Tethering: imagination is typically initialized from observed states. This creates a learning asymmetry, where the world model's manifold discovery outpaces the policy's sparse-reward optimization. We propose Mind Dreamer (MD), a framework that operationalizes Active Latent Intervention (ALI) to transcend Markovian continuity. MD reformulates discovery as the minimization of a global Relay Manifold Expected Free Energy (R-EFE); by sampling initial states from a learned generator $s_0 \sim p_{gen}(\cdot)$ rather than the historical buffer, MD utilizes an adversarial generator to synthesize non-continuous latent jumps to epistemic blind spots that are physically plausible yet cognitively challenging. To resolve the credit assignment paradox across these spatial ruptures, we derive the Relay Value Function (RVF) and Relay Uncertainty Function (RUF). These potentials treat synthesized anchors as counterfactual intermediary states, propagating pragmatic and epistemic value through a principled Bellman-style formulation. Notably, we prove that uncertainty propagation across discontinuities necessitates a quadratic discount $γ^2$, establishing a formal epistemic horizon. Theoretically, MD approximates a variance-minimizing importance sampler that expands the manifold's spectral gap, reducing the hitting time to critical bottleneck states. Empirically, MD achieves a 1.67$\times$ average speedup over DreamerV3 on DeepMind Control Suite, reaching 8.8$\times$ in sparse-reward tasks.

</details>


### [171] [Entropy Across the Bridge: Conditional-Marginal Discretization for Flow and Schrödinger Samplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16126)
*Bruno Trentini,Dejan Stancevic,Michael M. Bronstein,Alexander Tong,Luca Ambrogioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于条件-边际熵率的目标函数，用于桥感知离散化，从而为流匹配和薛定谔桥模型设计出无需训练的推理时调度器。在Gaussian布朗桥下该熵率具有闭式U形解，在2D桥/流模型、CIFAR-10的EDM以及AlphaFlow蛋白质生成任务中，均显著优于线性或余弦调度，尤其在小NFE低预算场景下表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定的小推理预算下，流生成模型的采样质量高度依赖于少量函数评估的分配位置，而现有的推理网格要么基于启发式设计，要么继承自一端扩散方法，缺乏理论指导。作者旨在从第一性原理出发，推导一个可分离桥几何与边际流演化的目标函数，以指导非均匀离散化。

Method: 推导了条件-边际熵率目标函数，将端点条件桥几何与边际流演化分离；在Gaussian布朗桥假设下得到闭式U形熵率分布；基于该分布设计非均匀推理调度器，无需额外训练即可应用于任意桥/流模型。

Result: 2D桥/流模型：10步ODE-Heun MMD提升18.1%，SDE-Heun提升22.7%；EDM/CIFAR-10：5步FID达186.3（线性200.5，余弦238.0）；AlphaFlow蛋白质生成：低NFE场景下在CAMEO22和ATLAS基准上均优于对比方法。

Conclusion: 熵率调度作为一种实用的低预算分配信号，能有效提升高维桥和流采样器在有限NFE下的生成质量，且无需额外训练成本。

Abstract: For a fixed flow-based generative model under a small inference budget, sample quality can depend strongly on where the sampler spends its few function evaluations. Flow matching and Schrödinger bridges define probability paths, yet their inference grids are usually heuristic or inherited from one-endpoint diffusion. We derive a conditional-marginal entropy-rate objective for bridge-aware discretization, separating endpoint-conditioned bridge geometry from marginal flow evolution, and use it to build a training-free entropic inference-time scheduler from first principles. For Gaussian Brownian bridges this rate is closed-form and U-shaped, motivating boundary-heavy nonuniform grids. On trained two-dimensional bridge/flow models, the estimated profile recovers the predicted shape and improves 10-step ODE-Heun MMD over linear by 18.1%, with a paired 22.7% SDE-Heun improvement in the same low-NFE sweep. On EDM/CIFAR-10, the entropic time-discretization gives the best tested five-step FID (186.3 \pm 4.0 versus 200.5 \pm 2.9 for linear and 238.0 \pm 5.3 for cosine). On AlphaFlow protein generation, entropic conditional-marginal (cond-marg) scheduling shows advantage in low-NFE regimes on both CAMEO22 and ATLAS benchmarks. These results support entropy-rate scheduling as a practical low-budget allocation signal for high-dimensional bridge and flow samplers.

</details>


### [172] [Navigating Potholes with Geometry-Aware Sharpness Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16134)
*Simon Dufort-Labbé,Mehrab Hamidi,Razvan Pascanu,Ioannis Mitliagkas,Damien Scieur,Aristide Baratin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLQR+SAM 结合了SAM和LLQR预条件器，通过双时间尺度机制（慢速学习几何+快速锐度校正）来增强对局部尖锐但全局平坦区域（potholes）的逃逸能力，在视觉和序列建模任务上优于单独使用SAM或LLQR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM对所有参数方向均匀扰动，忽略了损失几何的底层结构，导致在非均匀曲率区域效率低下。

Method: 将LLQR框架的预条件器（慢速指数移动平均更新）与SAM扰动（快速时间尺度）结合，形成双时间尺度优化方法。

Result: 在视觉和序列建模基准上，LLQR+SAM一致优于单独使用SAM或LLQR。

Conclusion: 慢速学习的几何信息与快速锐度修正确实具有互补性，预条件器能放大对"potholes"（局部尖锐但全局平坦的区域）的逃逸信号，同时保持宽平坦盆地的稳定性。

Abstract: Sharpness-aware minimization (SAM) encourages flat minima by perturbing parameters along directions of high loss curvature, but treats all parameter directions uniformly, ignoring the underlying loss geometry. We introduce LLQR+SAM, which combines SAM with a learned preconditioner obtained from the recently proposed LLQR framework, a second-order method that recasts steepest descent as a layerwise linear-quadratic regulator problem. The preconditioner is updated sparsely and maintained as a slow exponential moving average, so it captures a smoothed, low-resolution picture of the loss landscape geometry. The SAM perturbation then operates on top of this learned geometry, probing curvature at a faster timescale. We show that this two-timescale structure is not merely a computational convenience: theoretically, the preconditioner amplifies the SAM escape signal in directions that are flat under the average geometry but locally sharp (potholes). Wide, flat basins, by contrast, remain stable. Empirically, LLQR+SAM gives consistent gains over both SAM and LLQR alone across standard vision and sequence modeling benchmarks, supporting the view that slow learned geometry and fast sharpness correction are genuinely complementary.

</details>


### [173] [SAFE Quantum Machine Learning with Variational Quantum Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16067)
*Ying Chen,Paolo Giudici,Vasily Kolesnikov,Paolo Recchia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过幅度编码在高维深度表示上运行的变分量子分类器，结合可学习的经典预编码层和归一化量子观测，在安全性关键场景中实现了更平衡的SAFE可靠性轮廓，相比经典基线具有更好的噪声鲁棒性和特征移除稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索变分量子电路在安全性关键场景中的稳定学习机制，解决经典模型在鲁棒性和可解释性方面的不足。

Method: 采用幅度编码处理高维深度表示，设计可学习的经典预编码层进行稳定化；结合归一化幅度嵌入与有界量子观测，构建结构化的平滑假设类；使用基于Cramer von Mises散度的SAFE-AI指标进行多维度评估。

Result: 所提出的量子模型在预测性能上与强经典基线相当，同时在SAFE可靠性轮廓上更均衡，表现出更好的噪声鲁棒性和结构化特征移除下的稳定性。

Conclusion: 变分量子电路为安全性关键场景下的稳定性导向SAFE学习提供了原则性机制，具有良好的应用潜力。

Abstract: We propose a variational quantum classifier operating on high dimensional deep representations via amplitude encoding, stabilized by a learnable classical pre encoding layer.By combining normalized amplitude embeddings with bounded quantum observables, the resulting model induces a structured and smooth hypothesis class with controlled sensitivity to input variations. Model reliability is assessed using SAFE-AI metrics derived from the Cramer von Mises divergence, enabling consistent evaluation across accuracy, robustness, and explainability dimensions. Empirical results show that the proposed quantum model provides competitive predictive performance compared with strong classical baselines while exhibiting a more balanced SAFE reliability profile, with improved robustness to noise and stability under structured feature removal. These findings suggest that variational quantum circuits offer a principled mechanism for stability oriented SAFE learning in safety critical settings.

</details>


### [174] [ITGPT: Generative Pretraining on Irregular Timeseries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16069)
*Antoine Honoré,Ming Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ITGPT是一种基于注意力机制的架构，通过自监督学习和生成式预训练，能有效处理多模态、不规则采样的时间序列数据，无需重采样、特征融合或显式数据插补，在医疗和预测性维护任务上达到了SOTA性能，尤其在标签稀缺时表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时间序列回归模型难以充分利用大规模标注的多模态数据，尤其是当数据不规则采样或存在缺失值时。医疗和预测性维护等领域的数据来源不可靠，且标注需要专家知识或昂贵设备，亟需一种能高效处理此类数据的模型。

Method: 提出ITGPT架构，基于注意力机制，支持多模态、不规则采样时间序列；允许同时使用自监督学习（SSL）损失和类似GPT的生成式预训练目标进行训练，无需数据重采样、特征融合或显式插补。

Result: 在TIHM医疗数据集和CompX预测性维护数据集上达到SOTA性能；在标签稀缺时，通过SSL和GPT训练有效利用未标注数据，表现优于纯监督方法。

Conclusion: ITGPT代表了一种向高效利用大规模非结构化时间序列数据进行实际推理任务的重要进展，尤其适用于标签获取成本高的场景。

Abstract: Timeseries regression models often struggle to leverage large volumes of labeled multimodal data, particularly when the data are irregularly sampled or contain missing values. This is common in domains like healthcare and predictive maintenance, where data are collected from unreliable sources, and labeling requires expert knowledge or costly equipments. Transformer-based large language models have proven effective on structured data such as text through self-supervised learning (SSL) and generative pretraining (GPT) frameworks. However, such models lack the flexibility to efficiently process irregularly sampled multimodal timeseries data. In this paper, we introduce ITGPT, an attention-based architecture designed for handling multimodal, irregularly sampled timeseries by allowing training with both SSL losses and GPT-like objectives. We evaluate its performance on a healthcare task with the TIHM dataset, and a predictive maintenance task with the CompX dataset. Our results demonstrate that ITGPT achieves state-of-the-art performance without requiring resampling, feature fusion or explicit data imputation. Furthermore, when labels are scarce, ITGPT effectively leverages unlabeled data through SSL and GPT training, outperforming the purely supervised approach. This represents an important step towards efficiently using large and unstructured timeseries datasets for practical inference tasks.

</details>


### [175] [MIND: Decoupling Model-Induced Label Noise via Latent Manifold Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16081)
*Dayong Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MIND框架，通过潜在流形解缠将模型诱导的结构性标签噪声解耦为子空间依赖的可处理成分，在CIFAR-100、3D数据集(S3DIS, ScanNet)及视觉语言模型零样本幻觉校正中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练专家和基础模型驱动的自动标注范式引入了模型诱导的标签噪声，这种噪声与随机噪声不同，源于标注器的归纳偏差，表现为与局部特征流形紧密耦合的系统性误差。现有基于全局转移矩阵的方法无法拟合这些结构模式，而学习实例级转移矩阵在数学上不可解。

Method: 提出MIND框架，核心是Latent Decoupling Estimator (LDE)，通过潜在流形解缠将高维噪声流形解耦为子空间依赖的可处理成分。LDE动态将样本投影到具有一致错误模式的潜在结构聚类中，无需真实标签锚点即可实现噪声可识别性。

Result: 在CIFAR-100的受控噪声实验、S3DIS和ScanNet等大规模真实3D数据集的结构性压力测试中，MIND显著优于现有方法；有效校正了视觉语言模型(如OpenSeg)的零样本幻觉。

Conclusion: MIND为处理模型诱导的结构性标签噪声提供了理论严谨且实践有效的框架，展示了作为基础模型鲁棒蒸馏框架的潜力。

Abstract: The paradigm of learning from automatic annotations driven by pre-trained experts and Foundation Models dominates data-hungry applications. However, it introduces a critical challenge: model-induced label noise. Unlike stochastic noise in classical robust learning, this noise stems from annotator inductive biases, manifesting as systematic errors tightly coupled with local feature manifolds. Existing methods relying on global transition matrices underfit these structural patterns, while learning instance-specific matrices remains mathematically intractable. We propose Model-Induced Noise Decoupling (MIND), a theoretically grounded framework addressing this dilemma. We demonstrate that the high-dimensional noise manifold can be decoupled into tractable, subspace-dependent components via Latent Manifold Disentanglement. Specifically, our Latent Decoupling Estimator (LDE) dynamically projects samples into latent structural clusters with consistent error modes, facilitating noise identifiability without ground-truth anchor points. To rigorously evaluate robustness, we adopt a hierarchical protocol: moving from controlled noise on CIFAR-100 to a structural stress test on large-scale real-world 3D datasets (S3DIS, ScanNet), where error patterns explicitly couple with geometric manifolds. Empirically, MIND significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods on these complex benchmarks and effectively corrects zero-shot hallucinations from Vision-Language Models (e.g., OpenSeg), highlighting its potential as a robust distillation framework for Foundation Models.

</details>


### [176] [Entropic Auto-Encoding via Implicit Free-Energy Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16164)
*Hazhir Aliahmadi,Irina Babayan,Greg van Anders*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出熵自编码器（EAE），通过隐式先验和编码器集成缓解变分自编码器（VAE）的后验坍缩问题，在多模态潜在表征学习中展现出优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VAE普遍存在后验坍缩问题——潜在变量被忽略，原因是显式先验的施加将优化推向无信息潜在表征的区域。需要一种新的框架来解决这一根本性缺陷。

Method: 提出EAE框架：仅以重构损失为显式目标，通过自由能最小化的编码器集成隐式生成潜在变量的先验。集成偏置学习向近最优解的高容量区域，解码器更新引导搜索轨迹走向信息丰富的潜在表征。

Result: EAE能学习非高斯、多模态的潜在分布，生成多样且与数据一致的样本。在反应-扩散动力学捕获、MNIST隐式类别区分和CelebA人脸层次结构（从"全人类"脸到个体特征）上均表现出色。

Conclusion: EAE有效缓解了后验坍缩，保留了数据中的多种底层结构，在多个数据集上验证了其层次化、多模态潜在表征学习能力。

Abstract: Despite their ubiquity, variational autoencoders (VAEs) inherently suffer from posterior collapse, a failure mode in which latent variables are effectively ignored. This failure arises because explicit prior imposition drives optimization toward loss landscape regions corresponding to uninformative latent representations. Here, we introduce Entropic Autoencoders (EAEs), a framework in which reconstruction loss is the only explicit objective, and entropy generates the latent variables' prior implicitly through a free energy-minimizing ensemble of encoders. This ensemble biases learning toward high-volume regions of near-optimal solutions, while decoder updates direct the search trajectories toward informative latent representations. We demonstrate that EAEs mitigate posterior collapse by learning non-Gaussian, multimodal latent distributions that yield diverse, data-consistent generations and preserve different forms of underlying structure in the data. As a proof-of-concept, we show that an EAE captures a superposition of the known low-dimensional dynamics of a reaction-diffusion process. Then, we show that an EAE identifies implicit categorical distinctions in MNIST latent representations, and displays a hierarchical understanding of facial structure on the CelebA dataset, from an "all-human" face to individual-dependent features.

</details>


### [177] [BAPR: Bayesian amnesic piecewise-robust reinforcement learning for non-stationary continuous control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16170)
*Yifan Zhang,Liang Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BAPR将贝叶斯在线变化检测（BOCD）与鲁棒集成强化学习相结合，通过冻结信念分布的凸组合构建γ-压缩算子，在分段平稳控制系统中实现自适应保守性策略，并全部在Lean 4中形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际控制系统常处于分段平稳条件，标准鲁棒强化学习面临全局保守策略浪费性能与局部自适应策略在未检测到的模态切换时导致灾难性失败的根本困境。

Method: 提出BAPR（贝叶斯遗忘分段鲁棒SAC），核心包括：(1) BAPR算子——由冻结信念分布加权的模态条件贝尔曼算子的凸组合，证明为γ-压缩；(2) 利用Lean 4形式化验证压缩因子的锐利边界：当信念依赖Q函数时，压缩因子变为γ+λΔ，失败条件为γ+λΔ≥1；(3) 推导分量式形式化误差预算，约束切换后恢复；(4) BOCD驱动的自适应保守机制，检测延迟O(log(1/δ))；(5) 基于RMDM损失的上下文条件模块，训练时使用模拟器模态ID，部署时无需模态标签。

Result: 形式化验证显示：BAPR算子是γ-压缩算子；信念依赖Q函数时压缩因子为γ+λΔ，失败边界为γ+λΔ≥1；分量式误差预算约束切换后恢复行为；检测延迟O(log(1/δ))；全部结果在3个Lean 4文件中通过22条机器验证定理完成，共1,145行，无任何sorry。

Conclusion: BAPR通过冻结信念权重的凸组合设计，统一了变化检测与鲁棒强化学习，在理论可证明的收敛保证下实现自适应保守性策略，所有核心定理均经过完整的机器形式化验证。

Abstract: Real-world control systems frequently operate under \emph{piecewise stationary} conditions, where dynamics remain stable for extended periods before undergoing abrupt regime changes. Standard robust RL methods face a fundamental dilemma: a globally conservative policy wastes performance during stable periods, while a locally adaptive policy risks catastrophic failure when the regime changes undetected.
  We propose \textbf{BAPR} (Bayesian Amnesic Piecewise-Robust SAC), which unifies Bayesian Online Change Detection (BOCD) with robust ensemble RL. The BAPR operator -- a convex combination of mode-conditional Bellman operators weighted by a frozen belief distribution -- is a $γ$-contraction. A complementary counterexample, machine-verified in Lean~4, establishes a \emph{sharp boundary}: when beliefs depend on the Q-function, the contraction factor becomes $γ+ λΔ$ (where $Δ$ is the mode reward gap), and contraction fails exactly when $γ+ λΔ\geq 1$. We derive a \emph{component-wise} formal error budget for the abstract operator -- every component machine-verified -- bounding post-switch recovery; the budget applies to the abstract mode-mixture operator and inherits to the implemented shared-critic algorithm only through the frozen-parameter design intuition. All results are formally verified with no \texttt{sorry} (1,145 lines across 3 Lean~4 files, 22 machine-verified theorems). BOCD drives an adaptive conservatism mechanism: the policy becomes maximally conservative after detected change-points and smoothly relaxes as confidence grows, with detection delay $O(\log(1/δ))$. A context-conditioning module trained via RMDM loss provides mode-aware representations from simulator-provided mode IDs at training time and requires no mode labels at deployment.

</details>


### [178] [Imitation learning for clinical decision support in pediatric ECMO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16175)
*Fateme Golivand,Michael Skinner,Saurabh Mathur,Ameet Soni,Phillip Reeder,Kristian Kersting,Lakshmi Raman,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对儿科ECMO（体外膜肺氧合）治疗中决策建模的高复杂性和数据稀疏性问题，将临床决策转化为从轨迹中学习的模仿学习任务（动作未直接观测），采用TabPFN（基于Transformer的表格数据方法）与XGBoost、MLP等基线对比，发现TabPFN在真实儿科ECMO数据上持续优于传统方法，可作为临床行为基线用于决策支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿科重症监护涉及持续监测和生命支持治疗调整，建模这些干预措施对决策支持至关重要。而儿科ECMO领域面临高复杂性和数据稀缺的双重挑战，需要有效的建模方法。

Method: 将临床决策建模为从轨迹中学习的模仿学习问题（动作未直接观测），采用TabPFN（基于Transformer的表格数据方法）以及XGBoost、多层感知机（MLP）等传统基线，在真实儿科ECMO数据上学习动作模型。

Result: TabPFN方法在真实儿科ECMO数据上持续优于XGBoost和MLP等传统基线模型。

Conclusion: TabPFN可作为儿科ECMO决策支持中强有力的临床行为基线模型。

Abstract: Pediatric critical care is a dynamic, high-stakes process involving constant monitoring and adjustments in life-saving treatments. Modeling these interventions is crucial for effective decision support. To address the challenges of high complexity and data scarcity in pediatric Extracorporeal Membrane Oxygenation (ECMO), we frame clinical decision-making as learning to act from trajectories, i.e., imitation learning that learns action models from observational data, with a key feature that actions are not directly observed. We consider TabPFN, a recent transformer-based approach for tabular data, and traditional baselines including XGBoost and Multi-Layer Perceptrons(MLPs) on real-world pediatric ECMO data to learn the action models. We find that the TabPFN-based approach consistently outperforms these classical baselines, supporting its use as a strong clinician-behavior baseline for pediatric ECMO decision support.

</details>


### [179] [Hypothesis-driven construction of mesoscopic dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16211)
*Zhuoyuan Li,Aiqing Zhu,Qianxiao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于广义Onsager原理的介观动力学学习框架，通过在数学约束假设类中学习，为所有时空演化方程提供先验理论保证（全局适定性、渐近稳定性、唯一分解可识别性、离散能量耗散），并利用数据驱动实例识别，生成准确、鲁棒且可解释的动力学模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统科学建模从固定的、实例层面的有效方程出发，进行特定方程的分析与计算，在多尺度系统等复杂应用中极为困难。因此，需要一种替代范式来缓解传统方法的挑战。

Method: 基于广义Onsager原理，构建涵盖耗散和保守介观动力学的统一框架；在假设类中建立统一的先验理论保证，再通过数据驱动识别具体实例成员。

Result: 在连续PDE模型数据（验证）和微观链模型数据（真实介观模型未知）上进行了实证验证，该方法能有效学习动力学并提供可解释的底层物理诊断。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅是一种有效的动力学学习器，还提供了对底层物理的可解释诊断，具有准确、鲁棒和可解释的特点。

Abstract: Traditional scientific modeling typically begins with fixed, instance-wise effective equations and then carries out equation-specific analysis and computation, a procedure that becomes exceptionally challenging in complex applications such as multiscale systems. We propose an alternative paradigm by learning mesoscopic dynamics within a mathematically constrained hypothesis class. Building upon a generalized Onsager principle, we introduce a unified framework encompassing both dissipative and conservative mesoscopic dynamics. We establish uniform and a priori theoretical guarantees, including global well-posedness, asymptotic stability, unique factorization identifiability, and discrete energy dissipation, applicable to all spatio-temporal evolution equations within this hypothesis class prior to all learning stages. Data from each problem instance is then used to guide the identification of members within our hypothesis class, giving rise to accurate, robust and interpretable dynamical models. We empirically validate this framework on both data from continuum PDE models as a check, and on data arising from microscopic chain models for which exact meso-scale models are unknown. The proposed approach not only acts as an effective dynamics learner, but also offers vital interpretable diagnostics of the underlying physics.

</details>


### [180] [The Privacy Price of Tail-Risk Learning: Effective Tail Sample Size in Differentially Private CVaR Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16219)
*El Mustapha Mansouri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了差分隐私下CVaR学习的有效样本量、风险分解以及不同场景下的最优收敛速率，揭示了私有CVaR学习的核心困难子问题等价于在Θ(nτ)个尾部记录上进行普通私有学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解差分隐私如何改变CVaR学习的有效样本量，并建立完整的风险分解框架，从而刻画私有CVaR学习在标量估计、有限类以及凸Lipschitz学习等不同场景下的最优收敛速率。

Method: 理论分析：通过有效样本量概念（nτ替代n）建立隐私相关的尾部样本量（εnτ）；对纯DP和近似DP分别给出上下界；利用模块化约简方法将凸Lipschitz学习的私有CVaR问题归约到私有随机凸优化。

Result: 标量估计速率为Θ(B min{1, (nτ)^{-1/2} + (εnτ)^{-1}})；有限类（规模M）速率为Θ(B min{1, √(log(2M)/(nτ)) + log(2M)/(εnτ)})；凸Lipschitz学习中CVaR特定隐私项必然以1/(εnτ)尺度增长，维数依赖继承自私随机凸优化。

Conclusion: 私有CVaR学习的核心挑战可归结为在Θ(nτ)个信息性尾部记录上进行标准私有学习，CVaR超量风险可分解为普通尾部风险统计误差与隐私代价两部分，该分解在标量估计和有限类情形下是完整的。

Abstract: Differential privacy changes the effective sample size governing CVaR learning. For tail mass $τ$, the privacy-relevant sample size is not $n$, but $nτ$; equivalently, the effective private tail sample size is $εnτ$. Private CVaR excess risk decomposes into ordinary tail-risk statistical error and a privacy price. This decomposition is complete for scalar estimation and finite classes: scalar estimation has rate $Θ(B \min\{1,(nτ)^{-1/2}+(εnτ)^{-1}\})$, and finite classes of size $M$ have rate $Θ(B \min\{1,\sqrt{\log(2M)/(nτ)}+\log(2M)/(εnτ)\})$. These complete rates hold under pure DP, and their lower bounds extend to approximate DP in the stated small-$δ$ regimes. For convex Lipschitz learning, modular upper and lower reductions show that the CVaR-specific privacy term necessarily scales as $1/(εnτ)$, with dimension dependence inherited from private stochastic convex optimization. Together, these results identify ordinary private learning on $Θ(nτ)$ informative tail records as the canonical hard subproblem inside private CVaR learning.

</details>


### [181] [Dynamics-Level Watermarking of Flow Matching Models with Random Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16239)
*Shuchan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于动力学层面的生成模型水印方法，将水印嵌入到流匹配模型的连续动力学（速度场）中，通过密钥依赖的扰动实现消息编码，在不改变生成分布的前提下实现可靠的消息恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成模型水印方法主要嵌入到模型权重或输出中，缺乏在动力学层面进行水印嵌入的研究，需要一种不影响生成质量且具有密钥安全性的水印方案。

Method: 将水印嵌入到流匹配模型的连续动力学（速度场）中，通过连续信道上的随机编码实现：训练时添加密钥依赖的扰动，检测时通过黑盒查询恢复消息，并设计扰动以保持生成分布不变。

Result: 在MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上，不同架构下均实现了可靠的消息恢复，保持了生成质量，且没有密钥时解码准确率仅为随机水平。

Conclusion: 该动力学层面的水印方法能有效保护生成模型版权，在保证生成质量的同时具有密钥安全性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: We introduce a dynamics-level approach to watermarking generative models. Rather than embedding signals into model weights or outputs, we embed the watermark directly into the learned continuous dynamics -- the velocity field of a flow matching model. We formulate this as random coding over a continuous channel: a key-dependent perturbation is added during training, and the message is recovered at detection time from black-box queries. The perturbation is designed to leave the generated distribution unchanged. Experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 across different architectures confirm reliable message recovery, preserved generation quality, and chance-level decoding accuracy without the secret key.

</details>
